# Line up with Wealthfolio (1.62.0, Part 1) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The report counts spending exactly as Wealthfolio's Spending page does, and says so when it doesn't: new rows are written at noon UTC on the bank's date (W1); rows already stored at midnight UTC move to noon once (W2); the sync's bookkeeping rows are filed under an excluded "Sync bookkeeping" category (W3); every report figure reads one definition of spending, Wealthfolio's (W4); and after every sync a difference of more than a cent between the two month totals is a line in the daily report and on Home's Accounts tile (W5).

**Architecture:** Five pure shared modules. `shared/bank-day.ts` is the one helper every write built from a bank day goes through (`bankDayInstant`). `shared/wf-spending.ts` is the one definition of spending — Wealthfolio's classifier, allocation, exclusions and local-date placement — read by every report reader and by both sides of the audit (`shared/audit-headline.ts`). `shared/date-repair.ts` plans the one-time repair, `shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts` plans the bookkeeping filing, and `shared/spending-tripwire.ts` judges the two month totals the audit snapshot already carries. The companion reads rows, filings and split lines through one strict reader (`getNativeSpendRows` / `readSpendLines` in `companion/src/sqlite-native.ts`) on which every spending reader is rebuilt; it runs the repair (`companion/src/date-repair.ts`) and the filing pass (`companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts`) after each sync, before the cube; and its daily report gathers the tripwire as a finding printed in full (`companion/src/daily-findings.ts`). The app shows the same line on Home's Accounts tile.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, React (add-on), vitest (root project: jsdom, `src/` + `shared/`; companion project: node, `companion/src/`), Telegram Bot API (legacy `Markdown`), Wealthfolio REST and read-only SQLite (`node:sqlite`, the `sqlite3` CLI as fallback) on the companion, add-on SDK 3.6.2 on Wealthfolio 3.8–3.10.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-line-up-and-paybacks-design.md` — Part 1 only, rulings W1–W5 (approved by Nick 2026-10-14). Paybacks (W6–W10) and platform upgrades (U1–U4) are planned on other branches; W11 comes later. Branch `feat/line-up` at d344c96 (off main 71435fb = v1.61.0). The controller's rulings Q1–Q6 are folded in. Every line reference below is at d344c96; upstream references are `git -C ../wealthfolio-upstream show <tag>:<path>`.

**Verified facts this plan builds on (Q1):**
- **(a) Dates (W1).** Every write the sync, the companion and the add-on build from a `YYYY-MM-DD` — creates (`toActivityCreate`, `shared/sync-core.ts:3189`), the reconcile updates built on it (`toActivityUpdate`, `:3218`), the metadata, Amazon-label and legacy-placeholder updates (`:1206`, `:1253`, `:1318`), the baseline fix and the baseline move (`:880`, `:963`), the balance plug and both starting-balance corrections on the import endpoint (`:1586`, `:3588`, `:3681`), the relink creates (`:4099`), the payload recorded for a pruned duplicate (`recreatePayload`, `:1813`), linking and unlinking by re-creation (`shared/link-pair.ts:46`, `:134`), the fixes' write-back and tx-remove's recorded payload (`recreateWrite`, `shared/fix-row.ts:93`), and the Amazon/Venmo note Undo (`shared/change-undo.ts:699`, `:724`) — now sends `bankDayInstant(day)` = `${day}T12:00:00Z`. Updates are included because Wealthfolio stores an update's bare date exactly as a create's (3.8/3.9 `From<ActivityUpdate>`: 00:00 UTC, `crates/storage-sqlite/src/activities/model.rs:601-651` at v3.8.0), so a bare-date update would put a repaired row back at midnight. **A replay of what the change log recorded keeps its recorded date string:** Undo/Redo re-creating `before.activity` (`shared/change-undo.ts:612`, `:641`), re-importing a starting balance or correction (`:538`, `:568`, executed at `shared/change-undo-run.ts:363`), and moving a starting balance back to its recorded day (`:591`). The opening-debt filing writes no activity (`companion/src/opening-balance-category.ts:73-166` only assigns). Change-log entries keep a day: the plug and correction entries log the day, not the instant (`shared/sync-core.ts:3072`, `:3605`, `:3701`; `src/utils/sync.ts:459`). **Dedupe:** Wealthfolio's key hashes the activity's UTC calendar date only (`compute_idempotency_key`, `crates/core/src/activities/idempotency.rs:48-50`, identical at v3.8.0, v3.9.0, v3.10.0), so a noon write and a row stored at 00:00 UTC that day share one key; and the sync never plans that create in the first place — reconcile matches stored rows by the tx id in their note (`fetchExistingRows`, `shared/sync-core.ts:1142-1173`) and compares dates as days (`:1159`), so a 00:00 UTC row is neither re-created nor rewritten for its date alone (pinned in Task 1). **Readers:** every one takes the stored instant's UTC day — `toIsoDate` (`companion/src/rest-host.ts:26-30`, `src/utils/addon-host.ts:28-31`), `isoDay` (`shared/sync-core.ts:673`, `shared/fix-row.ts:56-58`, `src/utils/tx-story.ts:65-67`), `substr(activity_date, 1, 10)` and SQLite's `date()`/`strftime()` (UTC); Wealthfolio stores every instant as `…+00:00` (`model.rs:512-561`, `to_rfc3339` on `DateTime<Utc>`), so the UTC day and the first 10 characters are the same and no reader needs changing; the SQL bounds `activity_date >= 'YYYY-MM-DD'` hold for a noon row as for a midnight one. **Kept as sent:** 3.8 and 3.9's `validate_and_normalize_activity_date` (`crates/core/src/activities/activities_service.rs:1199-1219`) and 3.10's `validate_activity_timestamp` (`:1366-1383`) return a timestamp unchanged once its own date is supported. (3.10 alone stores a BARE date at the zone's local midnight, `:1343-1357` — which is why 3.10 rows already land on the right day.)
- **(b) The repair (W2).** Wealthfolio's search returns the stored date string (`ActivityDetails.date`) and the group (`sourceGroupId`), but the EFFECTIVE type — `COALESCE(activity_type_override, activity_type)` (`crates/storage-sqlite/src/activities/repository.rs:443-547` at v3.8.0; `effective_type_sql` at v3.10.0 `:550`) — with no override field (`ActivityDetails`, `activities_model.rs:850-891`). The update is patch-shaped for what matters: an omitted asset, amount, fee, quantity, unit price, tax, subtype, metadata, status, review flag, override and group are kept (`update_activity`, `repository.rs:865-979` at v3.8.0); filings live in their own table; a date change leaves split lines (`activity_update_invalidates_spending_splits`, `repository.rs:163-171` at v3.10.0). It is NOT patch-shaped for three fields: `activity_type` and `currency` are required, and the note is written as sent (`notes: domain.notes`, `From<ActivityUpdate>`, `model.rs:664` at v3.8.0 — an update without one clears it). So the repair sends `{ id, accountId, activityType, activityDate: noon, currency, comment }`, reads rows over REST as ruled, and asks the read-only database which rows carry an override (`getNativeOverriddenActivityIds`) — those, like grouped rows, are left alone, because an update would write the effective type as the stored one. Matching is exact: a bare date (3.8), `…T00:00:00Z` or `…T00:00:00+00:00` (fractional zeros allowed), in an account `account_mapping` maps. Batches of 25 with a 500 ms pause; a refused batch is retried one row at a time (a bulk write is all or nothing). The marker (`date_repair_done`) is written only when no row failed, so the next sync picks up the rest; one background bookkeeping entry per run carries the counts.
- **(c) Bookkeeping rows (W3).** By marker (`shared/audit-ledger.ts:80-83`) and type (`neutralAdjustmentFields`, `shared/sync-core.ts:1486-1510`; `startingBalanceFields`, `:1529-1560`), with Wealthfolio's bucket (`classify_activity_for_aggregation`/`classify_activity`, `crates/spending/src/activity_classification.rs:78-133` at v3.10.0, the same for spending at v3.8.0 and v3.9.0):

  | Row (marker) | CASH | CREDIT_CARD |
  |---|---|---|
  | In transit, money out (`isPlaceholderNote`) | TRANSFER_OUT → Expense | WITHDRAWAL → Expense |
  | In transit, money in | TRANSFER_IN → Income | TRANSFER_IN → Ignored |
  | Correction out (`isCorrectionNote`) | TRANSFER_OUT → Expense | WITHDRAWAL → Expense |
  | Correction in | CREDIT → Ignored | TRANSFER_IN → Ignored |
  | Starting balance (`isStartingBalanceNote`), negative / debt | WITHDRAWAL → Expense | WITHDRAWAL → Expense (filed under "Opening balances") |
  | Starting balance, positive / in credit | CREDIT → Ignored | TRANSFER_IN → Ignored |

  The existing filing: `fileCardOpeningDebts` files a card's opening debt under "Opening balances" and appends it to `spending.excluded_category_ids` only on a host that reports 3.9.0+ (`supportsExclusion`, `companion/src/opening-balance-category.ts:53-55`; `PUT /spending/settings`, `companion/src/wealthfolio.ts:363-370`; `/app/info`, `:350-359`), never for an account whose filing was undone (`noOpeningFile`, `:43-45`, `:95-96`), wired at `companion/src/index.ts:1066-1077`. The new pass (Task 5) runs right after it with the same three mechanisms: only spending-bucket rows (through `isSpendingBucket`, so a 3.10 card row the platform branch writes as TRANSFER_OUT — Ignored — is never filed, and the two compose), only with no spending filing (a row filed by hand, by a rule or under "Opening balances" is left alone), taken back out when the marker is gone (an expired placeholder), nothing at all below 3.9.0. **Linked re-creation drops the filing:** `linkPairByRecreate` deletes both rows and re-creates them under new ids (`shared/link-pair.ts:39-53`), and Wealthfolio deletes a row's filings with it (`activity_taxonomy_assignments … FOREIGN KEY (activity_id) REFERENCES activities(id) ON DELETE CASCADE`, `crates/storage-sqlite/migrations/2026-05-25-000001_spending_module/up.sql:46-57`); the new rows are a linked pair, never spending. The same migration makes one filing per (activity, taxonomy) unique (`:64`), so "double-filed" can only come from an older schema; the earliest-filing rule is kept as upstream has it.
- **(d) One definition of spending (W4).** Upstream, for `headline.spent` (`crates/spending/src/insight/service.rs` at v3.10.0): the accounts the page reads — `spending.enabled`, `spending.account_ids`, not archived, CASH or CREDIT_CARD (`:109-145`; `get_activities_by_account_ids` drops archived accounts, `repository.rs:1456-1469`); every row with `activity_date >= start && <= end`, the month in Wealthfolio's timezone (`:193-215`); per row `classify_activity_for_aggregation` by the effective type (`effective_activity_type`, `activities_model.rs:216-237` — 3.10 reads a blank override as none, 3.8/3.9 as itself, `:218-222`); a grouped TRANSFER_IN/OUT is InternalTransfer or Saving, never spending; `split_spending_allocations` — split lines win, else the whole amount to the earliest spending filing by `created_at`, then id (`activity_allocations.rs:39-92`), an allocation on an excluded category or any descendant removed, a row with no allocation Uncategorized and never excluded, refunds negative (`ExclusionIndex` and `split_spending_allocations`, `category_exclusions.rs:20`, `:99`; `insight/service.rs:597-726`); categories roll up to the top-level ancestor (`top_category_id`, `budget/service.rs:1231-1250`). FX conversion is not modelled (amounts are taken as stored). The readers of `spendingWhere` / `SPENDING_SIGN` at d344c96, and what each becomes: `getNativeWealthfolioSpendingBetween` (`companion/src/sqlite-native.ts:398`; the digest's month and week, `companion/src/index.ts:1907-1917`; the pool, `:1707`; the projection's history, `:1814`; the cube's check, `:1792`) and `getNativeWealthfolioSpending` (`:447`); `getNativeWealthfolioTopSpending` (`:474`, the weekly's biggest); `getNativeSubcategorySpending` (`:1085`, the digest's breakdown); `getNativeUncategorizedSpendingTotal` (`:1208`; digest `:2045`, pool, check); `getNativeSpendMatrix` (`:1320`), `getNativeUncategorizedByMonthAccount` (`:1495`), `getNativeMerchantRows` (`:1555`; also the forecast, strict, `companion/src/cash-forecast-facts.ts:54`), `getNativeDrillRows` (`:1653`) and `getNativeSpendDailyTotals` (`:1747`) — the cube (`index.ts:1773-1794`) and through it the forecast's daily spend and the pool's burn; `getNativeBudgetSpend` (`:1371`, suggested budgets, `companion/src/budget-suggest-host.ts:61`); budget vs actual (`companion/src/ledger-facts.ts:200-212`, its own copy of `SPENDING_SIGN`, `:22`); the audit's month read (`companion/src/sqlite-audit.ts:131-169`, `REPORT_COUNTS_CATEGORY`). All are rebuilt on `readSpendLines` (Tasks 2–3). Left as they are, with the reason: `countRulePatternMatches` (`:1275`, a rule preview's count, not a figure), the ledger audit's idle-refund finding (`ledger-facts.ts:121-131`), the needs-a-category list (`getNativeUncategorizedSpending`, `:612` — its filters decide what is offered for filing), and `companion/tools/audit-month.mjs:107` / `audit-headline.mjs:64` (hand-run scripts). The `\x1f` CLI path (`DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR`, `:1629`; the newline fix, `:128-136`) is kept: the new reader puts the note last and reads it with every newline made a space, and the real-sqlite3 test drives it (Task 3). After this the audit's five 1.61 reasons cannot arise; the agreement matrix pins each (Tasks 2 and 3).
- **(e) The tripwire (W5).** A2 already reads both figures after every sync: the cube's month (`monthlySpendTotals`, `companion/src/audit-facts.ts:156-162`) and Wealthfolio's `headline.spent` for the month in its timezone (`:175-186`), published in the audit snapshot (`publishAuditSnapshot`, `:234-247`, run last in `runCompanionSync`, `companion/src/index.ts:1229-1233`). The daily report gathers findings once for Brief and Full (`gatherFindings`, `companion/src/index.ts:2101-2105`) and prints in full every finding `printsInFull` says (`shared/self-check.ts:319-321`, `formatFindingsSummary` `:332-339`) — "⚠️ <message>". So the tripwire is a finding of a new kind, `spending-differs`, printed in full and without a key (never snoozed), judged from the latest snapshot: it clears itself on the first sync where the two agree, and a snapshot that cannot be read, a figure that was not read, or another month's snapshot say nothing. Home's status strip is the Accounts tile (`src/tabs/HomeTab.tsx:423-447`).

**Deviations from the spec (decided, do not "fix"):**
- **Three things now count that 1.61 left out, because Wealthfolio counts them:** a charge dismissed from the needs-a-category list (reversing 1.23.0's fix — a report that left it out would trip the wire every day), a charge whose transfer's other half is still on its way, and a withdrawal filed under a category named like "Transfers". All three stay off the needs-a-category list. On 3.8, which excludes no category, the sync's own bookkeeping rows count on both sides (Q1c: the pass does nothing there).
- **The cube's accounts stay the mapped ones.** A spending account the add-on does not map is counted by Wealthfolio and not by the cube; the audit names it as the one remaining reason, `not-covered` ("On accounts the add-on doesn't sync"), and the tripwire fires. The five 1.61 reason kinds stay in `HEADLINE_REASON_KINDS` so the app still reads a snapshot an older companion published; `explainHeadline` never finds them.
- **Updates are written at noon too**, not only creates (Q1a), and a recorded replay keeps its recorded date; change-log entries keep days.
- **The fake host reads a written date back as its UTC day**, as both real adapters do (`shared/fake-host.ts`), so tests that read rows back still see days.
- **The repair needs the database** to skip rows with a type override (the search returns only the effective type); without it, it waits for the next sync. It never writes a marker when nothing is mapped.
- **The bookkeeping pass reads the database directly** (`node:sqlite`, like the opening-debt filer), only in the accounts Wealthfolio's Spending page reads, skips a starting balance whose account's opening filing was undone (`noOpeningFile`), and records one entry per pass that covers creating and excluding the category.
- **The tripwire reuses the findings and the audit snapshot, and keeps no episode ledger:** the line is recomputed from the latest snapshot for every report, never said once, never snoozed.
- **Readers place rows on Wealthfolio's local day,** reading a day wider on each side; the merchant rows are now one per transaction (they were one per filing) and include an uncategorized refund as a negative amount; the drill rows' date is the local day.
- **Removed as dead:** the SQL spending pieces (`SPENDING_FROM`, `SPENDING_CATEGORY`, `REPORT_COUNTS_CATEGORY`, `SPENDING_SIGNED_AMOUNT`, `SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_EXCLUSION`, `spendingWhere`), the drill CLI parser (`drillRowFromCliParts`, `parseDrillCliLine`) and the month read's (`headlineRowFromCliParts`), `reportSpendSign`, and the dismissed-id plumbing in the pool's and the cube's dependencies. `getNativeBudgetSpend` takes no scope: it reads Wealthfolio's settings itself.
- **Copy the rulings did not spell out:** the app's line "The report and Wealthfolio differ by $X this month"; the reason "On accounts the add-on doesn't sync: Wealthfolio's Spending page reads them; the report covers only the accounts the add-on syncs"; the category description "Rows the sync writes for its own bookkeeping: transfers waiting for their other half, balance corrections, starting balances. Not spending."; the change-log summaries in Tasks 4 and 5.

**Task order:** the noon helper and every write (1); the one definition and the audit on it (2); every report reader on it (3); the repair (4); the bookkeeping filing (5); the tripwire in the daily report (6) and on Home (7); docs, the forced-date run and every gate (8).

## Global Constraints

- **Copy (verbatim):** Telegram `⚠️ The report and Wealthfolio differ by $X this month — /audit explains` (the finding's message is the text after `⚠️ `; `$X` is `changeMoney` of the absolute difference, e.g. `$951.45`) · app `The report and Wealthfolio differ by $X this month` · category `Sync bookkeeping` · reason label `On accounts the add-on doesn't sync`. Every other string is spelled out in its task.
- **Raw text in shared code.** Bank and account text stays raw in every shared line builder; Telegram escapes it (`escapeMarkdown`, `escapeFindingText`) and keeps it outside every entity. ⚠️ is the only glyph added, in Telegram only.
- **App.** No emoji in app copy (`\p{Extended_Pictographic}`; `src/pages/copy-policy.test.tsx`). No new CSS (the tile reuses `.sfin-tile-sub`). No new store method.
- **Writes only through existing routes.** Activities through the hosts' `saveMany` / `importActivities` and the REST bulk route; filings through `PUT`/`DELETE /spending/activities/{id}/assignments`; the category through `POST /taxonomies/categories`; exclusions through `PUT /spending/settings` (3.9.0+ only). The database stays read-only.
- **The change log never costs the work.** A failed append is logged and the repair or filing still stands.
- **Tests use a fixed `now`.** Shared and companion: fixed `Date`s and a `now` argument, or `vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] }); vi.setSystemTime(...)` with `vi.useRealTimers()` after; fixture databases set `timezone`. Every new or changed spending test file also passes with the date forced to 2027-01-01 (Task 8).
- **Imports:** shared sources import siblings WITH `.js`; `src/` imports shared WITHOUT an extension; companion imports `'../../shared/x.js'` and `'./x.js'`.
- **Run the companion suite from `companion/`** (`cd companion && npx vitest run …`), never with `--root`.
- **Gates before calling a task done:** the touched project's tests, plus `nice npm run type-check` when `src/` or `shared/` changed and `nice npm --prefix companion run build` when `companion/` or `shared/` changed. Final task: `nice npm test`, `(cd companion && nice npx vitest run)`, `nice npm run type-check`, `nice npm --prefix companion run build`, and the real-sqlite3 CLI test — all under `nice`: the Mac overheats.
- **Edits are exact.** Every "replace" block below is copied from the file as it stands after the previous tasks (and the earlier edits of the same file in the same task) and occurs exactly once; replace it verbatim (the Edit tool's `old_string` → `new_string`). Apply a file's numbered edits in order. Every "Create" block is the whole file.
- **No version bump, tag or release:** the release combines the branches. CHANGELOG goes under `[Unreleased]`.
- **Never `git stash`** (the stash stack is shared across worktrees). **Commits** end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

Each pinned by a test:

- **A noon write never duplicates a row already stored at 00:00 UTC** → no create, no update; the two share Wealthfolio's dedupe date. Task 1 (`never rewrites a row stored at 00:00 UTC for its date alone, and never creates it again`; `shares Wealthfolio’s dedupe date with a row already stored at 00:00 UTC that day`).
- **The repair skips grouped rows** (and rows with a type override). Task 4 (`skips a row in a group — a linked transfer keeps its date, and its partner’s`; `moves every mapped row at 00:00 UTC, skips linked legs, logs one entry and marks itself done`; `leaves a row with a type override alone`).
- **The repair runs once.** Task 4 (`runs once: a second sync reads the marker and touches nothing`).
- **The repair resumes after a partial failure** → no marker, the next sync moves only what is left. Task 4 (`after a partial failure, says so, leaves the marker unwritten, and the next sync moves only what is left`).
- **The repair never touches a row that is not at 00:00 UTC** (or not in a mapped account). Task 4 (`never touches a row at any other instant, or in an account the add-on does not map`; `… is not` cases of `utcMidnightDay`).
- **Bookkeeping: a placeholder is filed; an expired one is taken out; a hand-filed row is untouched; 3.8 does nothing; a non-spending-bucket row is skipped.** Task 5 (`files a placeholder under a new "Sync bookkeeping", excludes the category from spending, and logs one entry`; `takes out an expired placeholder, leaves a hand-filed row and one already filed alone`; `does nothing at all on Wealthfolio %s, which excludes no category`; `skips rows Wealthfolio does not count as spending, and rows in accounts its Spending page does not read`; and the pure plan's matching cases).
- **The agreement matrix: excluded categories, double-filed, uncategorized refund, first-midnight, a linked cross-boundary transfer, an activity type override (and the bookkeeping rows on 3.9 and 3.8)** → both sides equal, no reason. Task 2 (`the agreement matrix — …`, eight cases, through `explainHeadline`) and Task 3 (`the report counts each case exactly as Wealthfolio does`, the same eight through the real readers on a real database).
- **Tripwire: 1¢ → no line, 2¢ → a line; a failed read makes no claim; Brief carries it.** Task 6 (`agrees when the two are equal, or a cent apart either way`; `differs at two cents, either way, and says by how much`; `makes no claim when a figure is unread, or the comparison is another month’s`; `says the report and Wealthfolio differ, from the last sync’s checks — and nothing when they agree or the checks cannot be read (1.62)`; `carries the line in Brief and in Full, and archives it`) and Task 7 (`says nothing about the two when %s`).
- **Dates** → every test pins its clock; Task 8 runs every new or changed spending test file with the date forced to 2027-01-01.

### Task 1: A bank day is written at noon UTC

Ruling W1 (Q1a). One helper, `bankDayInstant`, and every write built from a bank day or a stored row's day goes through it: creates, reconcile updates, the metadata/label/legacy-placeholder updates, the baseline fix and move, the plug and both starting-balance corrections, the relink creates, the payload recorded for a pruned duplicate, link and unlink by re-creation, the fixes' write-back (`recreateWrite`, which tx-remove also records) and the Amazon/Venmo note Undo. A replay of what the change log recorded keeps its recorded date — `shared/change-undo-remove.test.ts`'s `puts the row back first, then takes it off the skip list` keeps pinning `activityDate: '2026-10-09'` unchanged. Change-log entries keep logging the day. The fake host reads a written date back as its UTC day, as both real adapters do. Fourteen existing expectations of a bare date in a write become noon.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/bank-day.ts`, `shared/bank-day.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/sync-core.ts`, `shared/link-pair.ts`, `shared/fix-row.ts`, `shared/change-undo.ts`, `shared/fake-host.ts`, `src/utils/sync.ts`
- Modify (tests): `shared/audit-link.test.ts`, `shared/change-undo.test.ts`, `shared/fix-row.test.ts`, `shared/sync-core-change-log.test.ts`, `shared/sync-core.test.ts`, `shared/tx-remove.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { bankDayInstant, BANK_DAY_HOUR_UTC } from './bank-day.js';
import { runSyncCore, VALUATION_POLL } from './sync-core.js';
import { createFakeHost } from './fake-host.js';
import { linkPairByRecreate, unlinkPairByRecreate } from './link-pair.js';
import { recreateWrite, type FixRow } from './fix-row.js';
import type { SaveManyRequest } from './sync-host.js';

/** Wealthfolio's dedupe key keeps only the activity's UTC calendar date
 *  (`compute_idempotency_key`: `activity_date.format("%Y-%m-%d")` on a
 *  `DateTime<Utc>`, 3.8–3.10). */
const wealthfolioKeyDay = (stored: string) => new Date(stored).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

describe('bankDayInstant', () => {
  it('writes a bank day at noon UTC', () => {
    expect(BANK_DAY_HOUR_UTC).toBe(12);
    expect(bankDayInstant('2026-10-01')).toBe('2026-10-01T12:00:00Z');
    expect(bankDayInstant('2027-01-01')).toBe('2027-01-01T12:00:00Z');
  });

  it('leaves a timestamp, and anything that is not a bare date, as it is', () => {
    expect(bankDayInstant('2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00')).toBe('2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00');
    expect(bankDayInstant('2026-10-01T12:00:00Z')).toBe('2026-10-01T12:00:00Z');
    expect(bankDayInstant('')).toBe('');
    expect(bankDayInstant('soon')).toBe('soon');
  });

  it('falls on the bank day from UTC−11 to UTC+11', () => {
    const at = Date.parse(bankDayInstant('2026-10-01'));
    for (let offset = -11; offset <= 11; offset++) {
      expect(new Date(at + offset * 3_600_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)).toBe('2026-10-01');
    }
  });

  it('shares Wealthfolio’s dedupe date with a row already stored at 00:00 UTC that day', () => {
    for (const stored of ['2026-10-01', '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00']) {
      expect(wealthfolioKeyDay(bankDayInstant('2026-10-01'))).toBe(wealthfolioKeyDay(stored));
    }
  });
});

    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] });
    vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
    VALUATION_POLL.delayMs = 1;
    VALUATION_POLL.attempts = 1;
  });

    accounts: [{

      transactions: transactions.map((t) => ({ ...t, posted: OCT_1 })),

  it('creates a new row at noon UTC on the bank date', async () => {

    });

    expect(saved.flatMap((s) => s.creates ?? []).map((c) => c.activityDate)).toEqual(['2026-10-01T12:00:00Z']);

  it('never rewrites a row stored at 00:00 UTC for its date alone, and never creates it again', async () => {
    const { host, store, saved } = createFakeHost({
      accountSet: feed([{ id: 'TRN-1', amount: '-12.50', description: 'KROGER' }]),
      mapping: { 'sfin-chk': 'wf-chk' },
      upstreamWriteRules: true,
      existing: new Map([['wf-chk', [{
        id: 'row-1', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'WITHDRAWAL', date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', amount: 12.5,
        comment: 'KROGER · TRN-1', sourceGroupId: null, subtype: null,
      }]]]),

    expect(result.imported).toBe(0);

  });

  it('updates a row the bank changed at noon on its date', async () => {
    const { host, store, saved } = createFakeHost({
      accountSet: feed([{ id: 'TRN-1', amount: '-14.00', description: 'KROGER' }]),
      mapping: { 'sfin-chk': 'wf-chk' },
      existing: new Map([['wf-chk', [{

        comment: 'KROGER · TRN-1', sourceGroupId: null, subtype: null,
      }]]]),

    await runSyncCore(host, store, { force: true });
    expect(saved.flatMap((s) => s.updates ?? []).map((u) => [u.id, u.activityDate, u.amount])).toEqual([['row-1', '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', 14]]);
  });
});

describe('re-creates from a stored row are written at noon', () => {
  const leg = (wfId: string, accountId: string, activityType: string) => ({
    wfId, accountId, txId: `TRN-${wfId}`, activityType, date: '2026-10-01', absCents: 5000, currency: 'USD', comment: `MOVE · TRN-${wfId}`,
  });

  it('linking re-creates both legs at noon on their dates', async () => {
    const requests: SaveManyRequest[] = [];
    await linkPairByRecreate(async (req) => {
      requests.push(req);
      return { created: (req.creates ?? []).map((c, i) => ({ id: `new-${i}`, accountId: c.accountId, activityType: c.activityType, date: c.activityDate, amount: c.amount ?? null, comment: c.comment, sourceGroupId: c.sourceGroupId ?? null })), updated: [], errors: [] };
    }, [leg('a', 'wf-chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT'), leg('b', 'wf-sav', 'TRANSFER_IN')]);
    expect(requests[1].creates!.map((c) => c.activityDate)).toEqual(['2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z']);
  });

  it('unlinking re-creates both legs at noon on their dates', async () => {
    const requests: SaveManyRequest[] = [];
    await unlinkPairByRecreate(async (req) => {
      requests.push(req);
      return { created: (req.creates ?? []).map((c, i) => ({ id: `new-${i}`, accountId: c.accountId, activityType: c.activityType, date: c.activityDate, amount: c.amount ?? null, comment: c.comment })), updated: [], errors: [] };
    }, [{ ...leg('a', 'wf-chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT'), newType: 'WITHDRAWAL' }, { ...leg('b', 'wf-sav', 'TRANSFER_IN'), newType: 'DEPOSIT' }]);
    expect(requests[0].creates!.map((c) => c.activityDate)).toEqual(['2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z']);
  });

  it('a fix writes a row back at noon on its date', () => {
    const row: FixRow = {
      id: 'a1', accountId: 'wf-chk', accountName: 'Checking', accountType: 'CASH', type: 'WITHDRAWAL', date: '2026-10-01',
      absCents: 1250, feeCents: 0, currency: 'USD', notes: 'KROGER · TRN-1', subtype: null, flowExternal: null, grouped: false,
    };
    expect(recreateWrite(row).activityDate).toBe('2026-10-01T12:00:00Z');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

**Create `shared/bank-day.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/bank-day.ts
 *
 * The instant a row dated by the bank is written at (1.62, ruling W1): noon
 * UTC on the bank's date, never a bare date.
 *
 * Why. Wealthfolio 3.8 and 3.9 store a bare `YYYY-MM-DD` at 00:00 UTC
 * (storage-sqlite activities/model.rs, `From<NewActivity>`/`From<ActivityUpdate>`).
 * West of UTC that instant is the previous evening, so Wealthfolio's Spending
 * page — which places a row by its LOCAL date — counted a row dated the 1st in
 * the month before (−$48.55 on 2026-10-14). Noon UTC falls on the bank's date
 * in every zone from UTC−11 to UTC+11.
 *
 * Every Wealthfolio version keeps a timestamp as sent: 3.8 and 3.9's
 * `validate_and_normalize_activity_date` and 3.10's `validate_activity_timestamp`
 * return the string unchanged once its own date is supported, and storage
 * normalizes it to `YYYY-MM-DDT12:00:00+00:00`. Wealthfolio's dedupe key hashes
 * the UTC calendar date only (`compute_idempotency_key`, "ignore time component
 * for matching", 3.8–3.10), so a noon write and a row already stored at 00:00
 * UTC on the same date share one key.
 *
 * Every reader in the add-on takes the row's UTC date (`toIsoDate`, `isoDay`,
 * `substr(activity_date, 1, 10)`, SQLite `date()`), which is the bank's date
 * for both instants, so no comparison changes.
 *
 * A write replayed from what the change log RECORDED keeps its recorded date
 * string: it is put back as it was, not re-dated.
 */

/** The hour, UTC, a bank-dated row is written at. */
export const BANK_DAY_HOUR_UTC = 12;

const DAY = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

/** `2026-10-01` → `2026-10-01T12:00:00Z`. Anything that is not a bare date —
 *  a timestamp already, or text that is not a date — comes back unchanged. */
export function bankDayInstant(day: string): string {
  return DAY.test(day) ? `${day}T${String(BANK_DAY_HOUR_UTC).padStart(2, '0')}:00:00Z` : day;
}
```

### Task 2: One definition of spending, and the audit on it

Ruling W4's core (Q1d). `shared/wf-spending.ts` transcribes Wealthfolio's Spending page: the accounts it reads, the effective type (a blank override is none), a linked transfer never, the earliest spending filing or split lines, excluded categories and their descendants (`excludedWithDescendants` moves here; `audit-headline.ts` re-exports it for its readers), an uncategorized refund netted, each row on its local day. `explainHeadline` reads both sides from it — Wealthfolio's in its spending accounts, the report's in the mapped ones — so the one reason left is `not-covered`; the five 1.61 kinds stay readable for an older companion's snapshot, and `reportSpendSign` goes. The reason matrix becomes the agreement matrix: each case 1.61 named, worked out by hand from upstream's rules, equals the report's figure with no reason. The companion's month read becomes the report's own strict reader, `getNativeSpendRows` (rows, filings oldest first, split lines; the note last on the `\x1f` CLI path, newlines made spaces), read a day wider on each side; `getNativeSpendingSettings` moves from `sqlite-audit.ts` (which keeps `getNativeAuditSettings` as its old shape) and adds every spending category's name; `readSpendLines` is what Task 3's readers add up. Three test files' headline fixtures move to the new row shape: the app's (`AuditViews.test.tsx`) now shows a reason exactly as a 1.61 companion published it, the snapshot round trip and `/audit`'s menu a `not-covered` reason.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/wf-spending.ts`, `shared/wf-spending.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/audit-headline.ts`, `companion/src/sqlite-native.ts`, `companion/src/sqlite-audit.ts`
- Modify (tests): `shared/audit-headline.test.ts`, `shared/audit-snapshot.test.ts`, `src/components/AuditViews.test.tsx`, `companion/src/sqlite-audit.test.ts`, `companion/src/audit-menu.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/wf-spending.ts
export const SPENDING_TAXONOMY = 'spending_categories';
export interface SpendFiling { id: string; categoryId: string; createdAt: string; source: string }
export interface SpendSplit { categoryId: string; absCents: number }
export interface SpendRow {
  id: string; date: string; accountId: string; accountName: string; accountType: string; type: string; typeOverride?: string;
  subtype: string; groupId: string; absCents: number; notes: string; filings: SpendFiling[]; splits: SpendSplit[];
}
export interface SpendScope { timezone: string | null; spendingAccountIds: readonly string[] | null; excludedCategoryIds: readonly string[]; categoryParents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>> }
export function effectiveType(r: Pick<SpendRow, 'type' | 'typeOverride'>): string;
export function wfSpendSign(r: Pick<SpendRow, 'accountType' | 'type' | 'typeOverride' | 'subtype' | 'groupId'>): -1 | 0 | 1;
export function isSpendingBucket(r: Pick<SpendRow, 'accountType' | 'type' | 'typeOverride' | 'subtype' | 'groupId'>): boolean;
export function excludedWithDescendants(raw: readonly string[], parents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>): Set<string>;
export function topCategoryId(categoryId: string, parents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>): string;
export function localDay(stored: string, timeZone: string | null): string | null;
export interface SpendAllocation { categoryId: string; cents: number; source: string }
export interface SpendLine { row: SpendRow; day: string; month: string; cents: number; allocations: SpendAllocation[]; uncategorizedCents: number }
export function spendLine(row: SpendRow, scope: SpendScope, excluded: ReadonlySet<string>): SpendLine | null;
export function spendLines(rows: readonly SpendRow[], scope: SpendScope): SpendLine[];
export function spentCents(lines: readonly SpendLine[]): number;
// shared/audit-headline.ts
export type HeadlineDbRow = SpendRow;
export type HeadlineReasonKind = 'not-covered' | 'first-midnight' | 'bookkeeping' | 'excluded' | 'double-filed' | 'uncategorized-refund';
export { excludedWithDescendants } from './wf-spending.js';   // reportSpendSign is gone
// companion/src/sqlite-native.ts
export const SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER = 256 * 1024 * 1024;
export function spendRowFromCliParts(parts: string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null;
export function getNativeSpendRows(dbPath: string, startDay: string, endDayExclusive: string): SpendRow[];   // strict
export interface NativeSpendingSettings { timezone: string | null; excludedCategoryIds: string[]; categoryParents: Record<string, string | null>; categoryNames: Record<string, string>; spendingOn: boolean; spendingAccountIds: string[] }
export function getNativeSpendingSettings(dbPath: string): NativeSpendingSettings;   // strict
export function readSpendLines(dbPath: string, startDay: string, endDayExclusive: string): { lines: SpendLine[]; settings: NativeSpendingSettings };   // strict, a day wider
export function topCategoryName(categoryId: string, settings: Pick<NativeSpendingSettings, 'categoryParents' | 'categoryNames'>): string;
// companion/src/sqlite-audit.ts — getNativeHeadlineRows reads a day wider; headlineRowFromCliParts is gone; AUDIT_CLI_MAX_BUFFER = SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER
```

**Create `shared/wf-spending.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  effectiveType, excludedWithDescendants, isSpendingBucket, localDay, spendLine, spendLines, spentCents, topCategoryId, wfSpendSign,
  type SpendRow, type SpendScope,
} from './wf-spending.js';

let seq = 0;
const row = (over: Partial<SpendRow> = {}): SpendRow => {
  seq += 1;
  return {
    id: `act-${seq}`, date: '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', accountId: 'wf-chk', accountName: 'PNC Checking', accountType: 'CASH',
    type: 'WITHDRAWAL', subtype: '', groupId: '', absCents: 1000, notes: `SHOP · TRN-${seq}`,
    filings: [{ id: `asg-${seq}`, categoryId: 'cat-food', createdAt: '2026-10-05T13:00:00Z', source: 'manual' }], splits: [],
    ...over,
  };
};
const NY: SpendScope = { timezone: 'America/New_York', spendingAccountIds: null, excludedCategoryIds: [], categoryParents: {} };

describe('wfSpendSign — Wealthfolio’s classifier (3.8–3.10)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['CASH', 'WITHDRAWAL', '', 1], ['CASH', 'TRANSFER_OUT', '', 1], ['CASH', 'FEE', '', 1], ['CASH', 'TAX', '', 1],
    ['CASH', 'CREDIT', 'REFUND', -1], ['CASH', 'CREDIT', 'REBATE', -1], ['CASH', 'CREDIT', 'REIMBURSEMENT', -1],
    ['CASH', 'CREDIT', '', 0], ['CASH', 'CREDIT', 'BONUS', 0], ['CASH', 'DEPOSIT', '', 0], ['CASH', 'TRANSFER_IN', '', 0], ['CASH', 'INTEREST', '', 0],
    ['CREDIT_CARD', 'WITHDRAWAL', '', 1], ['CREDIT_CARD', 'FEE', '', 1], ['CREDIT_CARD', 'INTEREST', '', 1], ['CREDIT_CARD', 'CREDIT', '', -1],
    ['CREDIT_CARD', 'TRANSFER_OUT', '', 0], ['CREDIT_CARD', 'TRANSFER_IN', '', 0], ['SECURITIES', 'WITHDRAWAL', '', 0],
  ] as const)('%s %s %s → %d', (accountType, type, subtype, sign) => {
    expect(wfSpendSign({ accountType, type, subtype, groupId: '' })).toBe(sign);
  });

  it('never counts a linked transfer, inside the spending accounts or crossing out of them', () => {
    expect(wfSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', subtype: '', groupId: 'g-1' })).toBe(0);
    expect(wfSpendSign({ accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD', type: 'TRANSFER_IN', subtype: '', groupId: 'g-1' })).toBe(0);
    // A grouped row that is not a transfer is classified as itself.
    expect(wfSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'WITHDRAWAL', subtype: '', groupId: 'g-1' })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('classifies by the effective type: a non-blank override wins, a blank one is none', () => {
    expect(effectiveType({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', typeOverride: 'WITHDRAWAL' })).toBe('WITHDRAWAL');
    expect(effectiveType({ type: 'WITHDRAWAL', typeOverride: '  ' })).toBe('WITHDRAWAL');
    expect(wfSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'WITHDRAWAL', typeOverride: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: '', groupId: '' })).toBe(0);
    expect(wfSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', typeOverride: 'TRANSFER_OUT', subtype: '', groupId: 'g' })).toBe(0);
    expect(isSpendingBucket({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'DEPOSIT', typeOverride: 'WITHDRAWAL', subtype: '', groupId: '' })).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('localDay — Wealthfolio places a row by its local date', () => {
  it('puts a row stored at 00:00 UTC on the 1st in the previous month west of UTC, and noon on the day itself', () => {
    expect(localDay('2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', 'America/New_York')).toBe('2026-09-30');
    expect(localDay('2026-10-01', 'America/New_York')).toBe('2026-09-30');
    expect(localDay('2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00', 'America/New_York')).toBe('2026-10-01');
    expect(localDay('2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', 'Pacific/Auckland')).toBe('2026-10-02');
    // 3.10 stores a hand-entered date at the zone's own midnight.
    expect(localDay('2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00', 'America/New_York')).toBe('2026-10-01');
  });

  it('reads an unknown zone as the process’s own, and an unreadable date as none', () => {
    const d = new Date('2026-10-05T12:00:00Z');
    const own = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    expect(localDay('2026-10-05T12:00:00Z', 'Mars/Olympus')).toBe(own);
    expect(localDay('2026-10-05T12:00:00Z', null)).toBe(own);
    expect(localDay('soon', 'America/New_York')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('spendLine — the allocation and the exclusions', () => {
  const excludedNone = new Set<string>();

  it('allocates a row filed twice to its EARLIEST filing, once', () => {
    const line = spendLine(row({ filings: [
      { id: 'b', categoryId: 'cat-late', createdAt: '2026-10-06T00:00:00Z', source: 'rule' },
      { id: 'a', categoryId: 'cat-early', createdAt: '2026-10-05T00:00:00Z', source: 'manual' },
    ] }), NY, excludedNone)!;
    expect(line.cents).toBe(1000);
    expect(line.allocations).toEqual([{ categoryId: 'cat-early', cents: 1000, source: 'manual' }]);
  });

  it('breaks a created-at tie by the filing’s id', () => {
    const at = '2026-10-05T00:00:00Z';
    const line = spendLine(row({ filings: [
      { id: 'z', categoryId: 'cat-z', createdAt: at, source: '' }, { id: 'm', categoryId: 'cat-m', createdAt: at, source: '' },
    ] }), NY, excludedNone)!;
    expect(line.allocations.map((a) => a.categoryId)).toEqual(['cat-m']);
  });

  it('lets split lines win over the filing, signed like the row', () => {
    const splits = [{ categoryId: 'cat-food', absCents: 600 }, { categoryId: 'cat-home', absCents: 400 }];
    expect(spendLine(row({ splits }), NY, excludedNone)!.allocations).toEqual([
      { categoryId: 'cat-food', cents: 600, source: '' }, { categoryId: 'cat-home', cents: 400, source: '' },
    ]);
    const refund = spendLine(row({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REFUND', splits }), NY, excludedNone)!;
    expect(refund.allocations.map((a) => a.cents)).toEqual([-600, -400]);
    expect(refund.cents).toBe(-1000);
  });

  it('nets a refund with no category against Uncategorized', () => {
    const line = spendLine(row({ accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD', type: 'CREDIT', filings: [] }), NY, excludedNone)!;
    expect(line).toMatchObject({ cents: -1000, uncategorizedCents: -1000, allocations: [] });
  });

  it('drops an excluded allocation, and its descendants, but never an uncategorized row', () => {
    const parents = { 'cat-school': null, 'cat-tuition': 'cat-school', 'cat-food': null };
    const excluded = excludedWithDescendants(['cat-school'], parents);
    expect([...excluded].sort()).toEqual(['cat-school', 'cat-tuition']);
    expect(spendLine(row({ filings: [{ id: 'x', categoryId: 'cat-tuition', createdAt: '', source: '' }] }), NY, excluded)).toBeNull();
    const split = spendLine(row({ splits: [{ categoryId: 'cat-tuition', absCents: 700 }, { categoryId: 'cat-food', absCents: 300 }] }), NY, excluded)!;
    expect(split).toMatchObject({ cents: 300, allocations: [{ categoryId: 'cat-food', cents: 300, source: '' }], uncategorizedCents: 0 });
    expect(spendLine(row({ filings: [] }), NY, excluded)).toMatchObject({ cents: 1000, uncategorizedCents: 1000 });
  });

  it('counts only the accounts the Spending page reads', () => {
    const scope = { ...NY, spendingAccountIds: ['wf-card'] };
    expect(spendLine(row(), scope, excludedNone)).toBeNull();
    expect(spendLine(row({ accountId: 'wf-card', accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD' }), scope, excludedNone)).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('places the line on Wealthfolio’s local day and month', () => {
    expect(spendLine(row({ date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00' }), NY, excludedNone)).toMatchObject({ day: '2026-09-30', month: '2026-09' });
    expect(spendLine(row({ date: '2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00' }), NY, excludedNone)).toMatchObject({ day: '2026-10-01', month: '2026-10' });
  });
});

describe('spendLines and spentCents', () => {
  it('sums what Wealthfolio’s headline counts: expenses less refunds, excluded categories left out', () => {
    const lines = spendLines([
      row({ absCents: 5000 }),
      row({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', absCents: 1400 }),
      row({ type: 'DEPOSIT', absCents: 99999 }),
      row({ absCents: 2500, filings: [{ id: 'x', categoryId: 'cat-skip', createdAt: '', source: '' }] }),
      row({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', groupId: 'g', absCents: 30000 }),
    ], { ...NY, excludedCategoryIds: ['cat-skip'] });
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(spentCents(lines)).toBe(3600);
  });

  it('rolls a category up to its top-level ancestor, bounded against a cycle', () => {
    const parents = { a: null, b: 'a', c: 'b', x: 'y', y: 'x' };
    expect(topCategoryId('c', parents)).toBe('a');
    expect(topCategoryId('a', parents)).toBe('a');
    expect(topCategoryId('gone', parents)).toBe('gone');
    expect(['x', 'y']).toContain(topCategoryId('x', parents));
  });
});
```

```ts
  explainHeadline, headlineAgreeLine, headlineAgrees, headlineReasonLine, headlineRowLine, headlineSummaryLine, headlineUnexplainedLine,

  type HeadlineDbRow, type HeadlineFacts, type HeadlineRead,
} from './audit-headline';

let seq = 0;
const row = (over: Partial<HeadlineDbRow> = {}): HeadlineDbRow => {
  seq += 1;
  return {
    id: `act-${seq}`, date: '2026-10-05T00:00:00+00:00', accountId: 'wf-chk', groupId: '', accountName: 'PNC Checking', accountType: 'CASH',
    type: 'WITHDRAWAL', subtype: '', absCents: 1000, notes: `SHOP · TRN-${seq}`, spendingFilings: 1, categoryIds: ['cat-x'],
    ...over,
  };

/** One row for each of the five reasons, a late-month charge both sides
 *  count, and an ordinary purchase — in New York, west of UTC. */
function octoberFacts(): HeadlineFacts {
  return {
    month: '2026-10',
    timezone: 'America/New_York',
    reportCents: 130000,
    wealthfolioCents: 120000,
    excludedCategoryIds: ['cat-excluded'],
    rows: [
      row({ date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', absCents: 2500, notes: 'RENT PORTAL · TRN-1' }),
      row({ date: '2026-10-20T00:00:00+00:00', absCents: 4000, notes: 'GYM · TRN-2' }),
      row({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', absCents: 600, categoryIds: [], spendingFilings: 0, notes: 'Balance adjustment · ACT-1 · 2026-10-02' }),
      row({ absCents: 1500, categoryIds: ['cat-excluded'], spendingCategoryId: 'cat-excluded', notes: 'TUITION · TRN-3' }),
      row({ absCents: 1200, spendingFilings: 2, categoryIds: ['cat-a', 'cat-b'], notes: 'SPLIT · TRN-4' }),
      row({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REFUND', absCents: 800, categoryIds: [], spendingFilings: 0, notes: 'RETURN · TRN-5' }),
      row({ absCents: 9999, notes: 'KROGER · TRN-6' }),
    ],
  };
}

describe('reportSpendSign', () => {
  it('is the report’s own sign: card credits refund, cash credits only with a refund subtype', () => {
    expect(reportSpendSign({ accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD', type: 'INTEREST', subtype: '' })).toBe(1);
    expect(reportSpendSign({ accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD', type: 'CREDIT', subtype: '' })).toBe(-1);
    expect(reportSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', subtype: '' })).toBe(1);
    expect(reportSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' })).toBe(-1);
    expect(reportSpendSign({ accountType: 'CASH', type: 'CREDIT', subtype: '' })).toBe(0);

```

with:

```ts
  explainHeadline, headlineAgreeLine, headlineAgrees, headlineReasonLine, headlineRowLine, headlineSummaryLine, headlineUnexplainedLine,
  zonedMonthBounds, HEADLINE_AGREE, HEADLINE_READ_NOTES, HEADLINE_REASON_KINDS,
  type HeadlineDbRow, type HeadlineFacts, type HeadlineRead,
} from './audit-headline';
import { spendLines, spentCents } from './wf-spending';

let seq = 0;
const filed = (categoryId: string, createdAt = '2026-10-05T13:00:00Z') => {
  seq += 1;
  return { id: `asg-${seq}`, categoryId, createdAt, source: 'manual' };
};
const row = (over: Partial<HeadlineDbRow> = {}): HeadlineDbRow => {
  seq += 1;
  return {
    id: `act-${seq}`, date: '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', accountId: 'wf-chk', groupId: '', accountName: 'PNC Checking', accountType: 'CASH',
    type: 'WITHDRAWAL', subtype: '', absCents: 1000, notes: `SHOP · TRN-${seq}`, filings: [filed('cat-x')], splits: [],
    ...over,
  };
};
const unfiled = { filings: [] };
const card = { accountId: 'wf-card', accountName: 'Visa', accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD' };

/** The report's "Spent this month" for these rows: the lines the companion's
 *  readers build the cube from (companion/src/sqlite-native.ts), in the
 *  report's accounts and month. */
function reportCentsFor(f: HeadlineFacts): number {
  const covered = f.reportAccountIds ? new Set(f.reportAccountIds) : null;
  const lines = spendLines((f.rows ?? []).filter((r) => !covered || covered.has(r.accountId)), {
    timezone: f.timezone ?? null, spendingAccountIds: f.spendingAccountIds ?? null,
    excludedCategoryIds: f.excludedCategoryIds ?? [], categoryParents: f.categoryParents ?? {},
  });
  return spentCents(lines.filter((l) => l.month === f.month));
}

/** New York: Wealthfolio's October starts at 04:00 UTC on the 1st. */
const october = (over: Partial<HeadlineFacts>): HeadlineFacts => ({
  month: '2026-10', timezone: 'America/New_York', excludedCategoryIds: [], spendingAccountIds: ['wf-chk', 'wf-card'], reportAccountIds: ['wf-chk', 'wf-card'], ...over,
});

describe('the agreement matrix — each rule 1.61 named a reason for is now the same on both sides', () => {
  // Wealthfolio's figure is worked out by hand from upstream's rules
  // (crates/spending insight/service.rs, activity_classification.rs,
  // activity_allocations.rs, category_exclusions.rs); the report's comes from
  // the shared definition. Each case used to differ by the amount in its name.
  it.each<[string, HeadlineFacts, number]>([
    ['excluded categories, and a descendant (used to differ by $15.00)', october({
      excludedCategoryIds: ['cat-edu'], categoryParents: { 'cat-edu': null, 'cat-tuition': 'cat-edu', 'cat-food': null },
      rows: [row({ absCents: 1500, filings: [filed('cat-tuition')] }), row({ absCents: 10000, filings: [filed('cat-food')] })],
    }), 10000],
    ['a row filed under two categories counts once, under its earliest filing (used to differ by $12.00)', october({
      rows: [row({ absCents: 1200, filings: [filed('cat-b', '2026-10-06T00:00:00Z'), filed('cat-a', '2026-10-05T00:00:00Z')] })],
    }), 1200],
    ['a refund with no category is netted (used to differ by $8.00)', october({
      rows: [row({ absCents: 5000 }), row({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REFUND', absCents: 800, ...unfiled }), row({ ...card, type: 'CREDIT', absCents: 300, ...unfiled })],
    }), 3900],
    ['a row stored at 00:00 UTC on the 1st is September’s west of UTC (used to differ by $25.00)', october({
      rows: [
        row({ date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', absCents: 2500 }),
        row({ date: '2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00', absCents: 1000 }),
        row({ date: '2026-11-01T00:00:00+00:00', absCents: 4000 }),
      ],
    }), 5000],
    ['a linked transfer out of the spending accounts is a saving, not spending', october({
      rows: [
        row({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', groupId: 'g-sav', absCents: 50000, ...unfiled, notes: 'TO SAVINGS · TRN-1' }),
        row({ accountId: 'wf-sav', accountType: 'CASH', type: 'TRANSFER_IN', groupId: 'g-sav', absCents: 50000, ...unfiled }),
        row({ ...card, type: 'TRANSFER_IN', groupId: 'g-pay', absCents: 9000, ...unfiled }),
        row({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', groupId: 'g-pay', absCents: 9000, filings: [filed('cat-x')] }),
        row({ absCents: 2000 }),
      ],
    }), 2000],
    ['an activity type override is the type Wealthfolio counts', october({
      rows: [
        row({ type: 'DEPOSIT', typeOverride: 'WITHDRAWAL', absCents: 3000 }),
        row({ type: 'WITHDRAWAL', typeOverride: 'DEPOSIT', absCents: 7000 }),
        row({ type: 'WITHDRAWAL', typeOverride: '', absCents: 100 }),
      ],
    }), 3100],
    ['the sync’s bookkeeping rows, filed under an excluded category (3.9+; used to differ by $10.00)', october({
      excludedCategoryIds: ['cat-sync-bookkeeping'], categoryParents: { 'cat-sync-bookkeeping': null },
      rows: [
        row({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', absCents: 1000, filings: [filed('cat-sync-bookkeeping')], notes: '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-9' }),
        row({ absCents: 2000 }),
      ],
    }), 2000],
    ['the sync’s bookkeeping rows on 3.8, which has no exclusions: both sides count them', october({
      rows: [
        row({ type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', absCents: 600, ...unfiled, notes: 'Balance adjustment · ACT-1 · 2026-10-02' }),
        row({ absCents: 2000 }),
      ],
    }), 2600],
  ])('%s', (_name, facts, wealthfolio) => {
    const report = reportCentsFor(facts);
    expect(report).toBe(wealthfolio);
    const h = explainHeadline({ ...facts, reportCents: report, wealthfolioCents: wealthfolio });
    expect(h.reasons).toEqual([]);
    expect(h.unexplainedCents).toBe(0);
    expect(headlineAgrees(h)).toBe(true);
  });
```

```ts
  /** New York: Wealthfolio's October starts at 04:00 UTC on the 1st. */

        row({ absCents: 10000, notes: 'KROGER · TRN-1' }),

        row({ date: '2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00', absCents: 5000, notes: 'RENT · TRN-2' }),
        row({ ...card, absCents: 800, ...uncategorized, notes: 'UNFILED · TRN-3' }),
        // A linked card payment: neither side's spending.
        row({ date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', groupId: 'g1', absCents: 150000, ...uncategorized, notes: 'AUTOPAY · TRN-4' }),
        row({ ...card, date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', type: 'TRANSFER_IN', groupId: 'g1', absCents: 150000, ...uncategorized, notes: 'PAYMENT · TRN-5' }),
        // An account neither side reads.
        row({ accountId: 'wf-other', date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', absCents: 700, notes: 'ELSEWHERE · TRN-6' }),
        // A placeholder at midnight UTC on the 1st: the report drops it by its marker, Wealthfolio counts it in September.
        row({ date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', absCents: 300, ...uncategorized, notes: '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-7' }),
        // A cash starting balance moves no spending on either side.
        row({ type: 'DEPOSIT', absCents: 120000, ...uncategorized, notes: 'Starting balance · sfin-chk' }),
      ],

      ],

    expect(h.unexplainedCents).toBe(0);
    expect(headlineUnexplainedLine(h)).toBeNull();

        row({ ...card, date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', type: 'TRANSFER_IN', groupId: 'g-autopay', absCents: 150000, ...uncategorized, notes: 'PAYMENT · TRN-2' }),
        row({ date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', absCents: 2000, notes: 'PARKING · TRN-3' }),
        row({ absCents: 10000, notes: 'KROGER · TRN-4' }),

    expect(h.reasons).toEqual([{ kind: 'first-midnight', cents: 2000, rows: [expect.objectContaining({ description: 'PARKING', cents: 2000 })] }]);
    expect(h.unexplainedCents).toBe(0);

  it('(d) east of UTC, midnight UTC on the 1st is already the 1st there: no reason', () => {
```

**Create `shared/wf-spending.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/wf-spending.ts
 *
 * What counts as spending — ONE definition, Wealthfolio's (1.62, ruling W4).
 * The report (the cube, the digest, the weekly and pool figures, budget vs
 * actual, the forecast, suggested budgets) and the audit's model of
 * Wealthfolio's Spending page both read it from here, so the two cannot drift.
 *
 * Transcribed from upstream crates/spending (identical for spending on 3.8,
 * 3.9 and 3.10):
 *  - insight/service.rs `compute`: the accounts its Spending page reads
 *    (`spending.account_ids`, not archived, CASH or CREDIT_CARD), every row in
 *    the window `activity_date >= start && <= end` — the calendar month in
 *    Wealthfolio's timezone, so a row is placed by its LOCAL date;
 *  - activity_classification.rs `classify_activity_for_aggregation`: by the
 *    EFFECTIVE type (a non-blank `activity_type_override` wins), the subtype
 *    and the account type. A linked TRANSFER_IN/OUT (one with a group) is
 *    never spending: an internal transfer, or a saving when it crosses out of
 *    the spending accounts. An unlinked one falls through to the classifier,
 *    where a cash TRANSFER_OUT is an expense;
 *  - activity_allocations.rs `allocations_for_taxonomy`: split lines win when
 *    there are any; otherwise the whole amount goes to the EARLIEST spending
 *    filing (by `created_at`, then id) — a row filed twice counts once;
 *  - category_exclusions.rs `split_spending_allocations`: an allocation on an
 *    excluded category or any descendant leaves spending; a row with no
 *    allocation at all is Uncategorized and is never excluded — a refund
 *    included, which nets against Uncategorized.
 * Amounts are taken as stored, in the row's own currency: Wealthfolio converts
 * a foreign-currency row to the base currency, which this does not model.
 */

export const SPENDING_TAXONOMY = 'spending_categories';

/** One spending-taxonomy filing (`activity_taxonomy_assignments`). */
export interface SpendFiling {
  /** The assignment's id: the tie-break after `createdAt`. */
  id: string;
  categoryId: string;
  /** `created_at` as stored. */
  createdAt: string;
  /** `source` ('manual', 'rule', …); '' when unrecorded. */
  source: string;
}

/** One spending-taxonomy split line (`spending_activity_splits`). */
export interface SpendSplit {
  categoryId: string;
  /** The line's amount, positive, in cents. */
  absCents: number;
}

/** One activity, as the readers hand it over. */
export interface SpendRow {
  id: string;
  /** `activity_date` as stored, e.g. `2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00`. */
  date: string;
  accountId: string;
  accountName: string;
  /** Upper-case. */
  accountType: string;
  /** `activity_type`, upper-case. */
  type: string;
  /** `activity_type_override` as stored; '' or absent when none. */
  typeOverride?: string;
  /** Upper-case; '' when none. */
  subtype: string;
  /** `source_group_id`; '' when none: a linked transfer's group. */
  groupId: string;
  absCents: number;
  /** The stored note, raw. */
  notes: string;
  /** Every spending-taxonomy filing. */
  filings: SpendFiling[];
  /** Every spending-taxonomy split line. */
  splits: SpendSplit[];
}

/** Wealthfolio's Spending settings, as its page applies them. */
export interface SpendScope {
  /** Wealthfolio's timezone; null: the process's own. */
  timezone: string | null;
  /** The accounts the Spending page reads; null when unread: every account. */
  spendingAccountIds: readonly string[] | null;
  /** `spending.excluded_category_ids`; [] when unset (3.8). */
  excludedCategoryIds: readonly string[];
  /** Every spending category's parent (null at the top). */
  categoryParents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>;
}

/** The type Wealthfolio computes with: the override when it is not blank. */
export function effectiveType(r: Pick<SpendRow, 'type' | 'typeOverride'>): string {
  const override = (r.typeOverride ?? '').trim();
  return (override || r.type).toUpperCase();
}

const CASH_EXPENSE = new Set(['WITHDRAWAL', 'TRANSFER_OUT', 'FEE', 'TAX']);
const CARD_EXPENSE = new Set(['WITHDRAWAL', 'FEE', 'INTEREST']);
const REFUND_SUBTYPES = new Set(['REFUND', 'REBATE', 'REIMBURSEMENT']);
const TRANSFER_TYPES = new Set(['TRANSFER_IN', 'TRANSFER_OUT']);

/** `+1` an expense, `-1` money back against spending, `0` not spending. */
export function wfSpendSign(r: Pick<SpendRow, 'accountType' | 'type' | 'typeOverride' | 'subtype' | 'groupId'>): -1 | 0 | 1 {
  const t = effectiveType(r);
  if (TRANSFER_TYPES.has(t) && r.groupId !== '') return 0;
  const account = r.accountType.toUpperCase();
  if (account === 'CASH') {
    if (CASH_EXPENSE.has(t)) return 1;
    return t === 'CREDIT' && REFUND_SUBTYPES.has(r.subtype.toUpperCase()) ? -1 : 0;
  }
  if (account === 'CREDIT_CARD') {
    if (CARD_EXPENSE.has(t)) return 1;
    return t === 'CREDIT' ? -1 : 0;
  }
  return 0;
}

/** Whether Wealthfolio puts the row in the spending bucket at all — the only
 *  rows a spending category can be filed on. */
export function isSpendingBucket(r: Pick<SpendRow, 'accountType' | 'type' | 'typeOverride' | 'subtype' | 'groupId'>): boolean {
  return wfSpendSign(r) !== 0;
}

/**
 * The excluded categories and every descendant, as Wealthfolio's
 * ExclusionIndex builds them: the raw ids are kept even when the category no
 * longer exists, and the walk up each parent chain is bounded (32 steps), so
 * a cyclic taxonomy cannot hang it.
 */
export function excludedWithDescendants(raw: readonly string[], parents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>): Set<string> {
  const excluded = new Set(raw);
  if (excluded.size === 0) return excluded;
  const roots = new Set(raw);
  for (const id of Object.keys(parents)) {
    if (roots.has(id)) continue;
    const seen = new Set([id]);
    let current = id;
    for (let depth = 0; depth < 32; depth++) {
      const parent = parents[current];
      if (!parent || seen.has(parent)) break;
      if (roots.has(parent)) {
        excluded.add(id);
        break;
      }
      seen.add(parent);
      current = parent;
    }
  }
  return excluded;
}

/** A category's top-level ancestor, as Wealthfolio rolls spending up
 *  (`top_category_id`: bounded at 32 steps, stops at a cycle or a parent it
 *  does not know). */
export function topCategoryId(categoryId: string, parents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>): string {
  let current = categoryId;
  const seen = new Set([current]);
  for (let depth = 0; depth < 32; depth++) {
    if (!(current in parents)) break;
    const parent = parents[current];
    if (!parent || seen.has(parent)) break;
    seen.add(parent);
    current = parent;
  }
  return current;
}

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

/** The calendar day a stored instant falls on in `timeZone` — Wealthfolio's
 *  local date. Null or a zone the runtime does not know: the process's own.
 *  A bare `YYYY-MM-DD` is the UTC midnight 3.8 stores it at. Null when the
 *  date cannot be read. */
export function localDay(stored: string, timeZone: string | null): string | null {
  const at = Date.parse(stored);
  if (Number.isNaN(at)) return null;
  if (timeZone) {
    try {
      const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(new Date(at));
      const p = Object.fromEntries(parts.map((x) => [x.type, x.value]));
      return `${p.year}-${p.month}-${p.day}`;
    } catch {
      /* an unknown zone: the process's own, below */
    }
  }
  const d = new Date(at);
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
}

/** One allocation of a row's spend to a category it counts under. */
export interface SpendAllocation {
  /** The category the money lands on (a split's or the filing's — the leaf). */
  categoryId: string;
  /** Signed cents: positive spend, negative money back. */
  cents: number;
  /** The filing's source; '' for a split line. */
  source: string;
}

/** One row as Wealthfolio's Spending page counts it. */
export interface SpendLine {
  row: SpendRow;
  /** Wealthfolio's local day, YYYY-MM-DD. */
  day: string;
  /** YYYY-MM. */
  month: string;
  /** What the row adds to the month's spent figure: its spend less any
   *  excluded allocation. Signed. */
  cents: number;
  /** Its allocations to categories that are not excluded. */
  allocations: SpendAllocation[];
  /** Its spend when it has no spending allocation at all (Uncategorized,
   *  refunds negative); 0 otherwise. */
  uncategorizedCents: number;
}

/** The row's spending line, or null when Wealthfolio's Spending page does not
 *  count it: an account it does not read, a type that is not spending, a
 *  spend wholly in excluded categories, or a date it cannot read. */
export function spendLine(row: SpendRow, scope: SpendScope, excluded: ReadonlySet<string>): SpendLine | null {
  if (scope.spendingAccountIds && !scope.spendingAccountIds.includes(row.accountId)) return null;
  const sign = wfSpendSign(row);
  if (sign === 0 || row.absCents === 0) return null;
  const bucket = sign * row.absCents;
  let all: SpendAllocation[];
  if (row.splits.length > 0) {
    all = row.splits.map((s) => ({ categoryId: s.categoryId, cents: sign * Math.abs(s.absCents), source: '' }));
  } else {
    const first = [...row.filings].sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? -1 : a.createdAt > b.createdAt ? 1 : a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0))[0];
    all = first ? [{ categoryId: first.categoryId, cents: bucket, source: first.source }] : [];
  }
  let excludedCents = 0;
  const allocations = all.filter((a) => {
    if (!excluded.has(a.categoryId)) return true;
    excludedCents += a.cents;
    return false;
  });
  const cents = bucket - excludedCents;
  if (cents === 0) return null;
  const day = localDay(row.date, scope.timezone);
  if (!day) return null;
  return { row, day, month: day.slice(0, 7), cents, allocations, uncategorizedCents: all.length === 0 ? bucket : 0 };
}

/** Every row's spending line, in the rows' order. */
export function spendLines(rows: readonly SpendRow[], scope: SpendScope): SpendLine[] {
  const excluded = excludedWithDescendants(scope.excludedCategoryIds, scope.categoryParents);
  const out: SpendLine[] = [];
  for (const row of rows) {
    const line = spendLine(row, scope, excluded);
    if (line) out.push(line);
  }
  return out;
}

/** The spent figure of the lines: Wealthfolio's `headline.spent`, in cents. */
export function spentCents(lines: readonly SpendLine[]): number {
  return lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.cents, 0);
}
```

```ts
const isDay = (s: string) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s);

/** The report's month of activities, every account, as the cube reads it:
 *  `[startInclusive, endExclusive)` on the stored date, both YYYY-MM-DD. A
 *  row's spending filings are the ones the report counts (spendingWhere: not
 *  rolled up to a transfer-named category). */
export function getNativeHeadlineRows(dbPath: string, startInclusive: string, endExclusive: string): HeadlineDbRow[] {
  // Interpolated (the CLI fallback cannot bind), so validated, never trusted.
  if (!isDay(startInclusive) || !isDay(endExclusive)) throw new Error(`malformed month bounds: ${startInclusive}..${endExclusive}`);
  const rows = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath,
    'audit month',
    `SELECT a.id AS id, a.activity_date AS activity_date, COALESCE(a.account_id, '') AS account_id,
            COALESCE(a.source_group_id, '') AS grp, UPPER(COALESCE(acc.account_type, '')) AS account_type,
            UPPER(COALESCE(a.activity_type, '')) AS activity_type, UPPER(COALESCE(a.subtype, '')) AS subtype,
            ROUND(ABS(CAST(COALESCE(a.amount, '0') AS REAL)) * 100) AS abs_cents,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM activity_taxonomy_assignments ata JOIN taxonomy_categories tc ON ata.category_id = tc.id
              LEFT JOIN taxonomy_categories parent ON tc.parent_id = parent.id
              WHERE ata.activity_id = a.id AND tc.taxonomy_id = 'spending_categories' AND ${REPORT_COUNTS_CATEGORY}) AS spending_filings,
            COALESCE((SELECT group_concat(ata.category_id, ',') FROM activity_taxonomy_assignments ata
              WHERE ata.activity_id = a.id), '') AS category_ids,
            COALESCE((SELECT ata.category_id FROM activity_taxonomy_assignments ata
              WHERE ata.activity_id = a.id AND ata.taxonomy_id = 'spending_categories'
              ORDER BY ata.created_at, ata.id LIMIT 1), '') AS spending_category,
            COALESCE(acc.name, '') AS account_name,
            ${NOTES} AS notes
     FROM activities a JOIN accounts acc ON a.account_id = acc.id
     WHERE a.activity_date >= '${startInclusive}' AND a.activity_date < '${endExclusive}'
     ORDER BY a.activity_date, a.id;`,
    headlineRowFromCliParts,
    SEP,
    AUDIT_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  return rows.map((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    const ids = String(v[9] ?? '');
    return {
      id: String(v[0]), date: String(v[1]), accountId: String(v[2] ?? ''), groupId: String(v[3] ?? ''),
      accountType: String(v[4] ?? ''), type: String(v[5] ?? ''), subtype: String(v[6] ?? ''),
      absCents: Math.round(Number(v[7]) || 0), spendingFilings: Number(v[8]) || 0,
      categoryIds: ids ? ids.split(',') : [], spendingCategoryId: String(v[10] ?? '') || null,
      accountName: String(v[11] ?? ''), notes: String(v[12] ?? ''),
    };
  });
}
```

```ts
const isDay = (s: string) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s);
const shiftDay = (day: string, days: number) => new Date(Date.parse(`${day}T00:00:00Z`) + days * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

/** The month's activities, every account, as shared/wf-spending.ts counts
 *  them: `[startInclusive, endExclusive)` on the stored date (both
 *  YYYY-MM-DD) read a day wider on each side, since Wealthfolio places a row
 *  by its local date. Each with its filings and split lines
 *  (`getNativeSpendRows`). */
export function getNativeHeadlineRows(dbPath: string, startInclusive: string, endExclusive: string): HeadlineDbRow[] {
  // Interpolated (the CLI fallback cannot bind), so validated, never trusted.
  if (!isDay(startInclusive) || !isDay(endExclusive)) throw new Error(`malformed month bounds: ${startInclusive}..${endExclusive}`);
  return getNativeSpendRows(dbPath, shiftDay(startInclusive, -1), shiftDay(endExclusive, 1));
}
```
