```ts
/** The sqlite3 CLI's output buffer for the spending reads (the audits' too):
 *  they return every row of a window, past execFileSync's 1 MB default. */
export const SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER = 256 * 1024 * 1024;
/** A note with each CR and LF made a space: the CLI prints a row per line. */
const SPEND_NOTES = `REPLACE(REPLACE(COALESCE(a.notes, ''), char(13), ' '), char(10), ' ')`;
const isSpendDay = (s: string) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s);
const spendTail = (parts: string[], from: number) => parts.slice(from).join(DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR);
const spendTrue = (v: unknown) => String(v) === '1' || String(v).toLowerCase() === 'true';

/** CLI line → the spending row read's nine columns (the note last). */
export function spendRowFromCliParts(parts: string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (parts.length < 9 || !Number.isFinite(parseFloat(parts[6]))) return null;
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) out[`c${i}`] = parts[i];
  out.c8 = spendTail(parts, 8);
  return out;
}

/**
 * Every activity whose STORED date is in `[startDay, endDayExclusive)` (both
 * YYYY-MM-DD), with its account and every spending-taxonomy filing and split
 * line — the rows shared/wf-spending.ts counts. A caller placing rows by
 * Wealthfolio's local date reads a day wider on each side (`readSpendLines`).
 *
 * Strict: a database that cannot be read throws NativeDbUnreadableError, never
 * "nothing spent". Every table and column read is Wealthfolio's own on 3.8,
 * 3.9 and 3.10 (`spending_activity_splits` arrived with the spending module,
 * 3.8). The sqlite3 CLI fallback splits on the unit separator, with the free
 * text last; the account names are their own read for the same reason.
 */
export function getNativeSpendRows(dbPath: string, startDay: string, endDayExclusive: string): SpendRow[] {
  // Interpolated (the CLI fallback cannot bind), so validated, never trusted.
  if (!isSpendDay(startDay) || !isSpendDay(endDayExclusive)) throw new Error(`malformed spending bounds: ${startDay}..${endDayExclusive}`);
  const window = `a.activity_date >= '${startDay}' AND a.activity_date < '${endDayExclusive}'`;
  const accounts = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending accounts',
    `SELECT acc.id AS id, UPPER(COALESCE(acc.account_type, '')) AS account_type, COALESCE(acc.name, '') AS name FROM accounts acc ORDER BY acc.id;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 3 ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: spendTail(parts, 2) } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const accountById = new Map(accounts.map((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    return [String(v[0]), { type: String(v[1] ?? ''), name: String(v[2] ?? '') }];
  }));
  const activities = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending rows',
    `SELECT a.id AS id, a.activity_date AS activity_date, COALESCE(a.account_id, '') AS account_id,
            UPPER(COALESCE(a.activity_type, '')) AS activity_type, COALESCE(a.activity_type_override, '') AS type_override,
            UPPER(COALESCE(a.subtype, '')) AS subtype, ROUND(ABS(CAST(COALESCE(a.amount, '0') AS REAL)) * 100) AS abs_cents,
            COALESCE(a.source_group_id, '') AS grp, ${SPEND_NOTES} AS notes
     FROM activities a
     WHERE ${window}
     ORDER BY a.activity_date, a.id;`,
    spendRowFromCliParts, DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const filings = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending filings',
    `SELECT ata.activity_id AS activity_id, COALESCE(ata.id, '') AS id, ata.category_id AS category_id,
            COALESCE(ata.created_at, '') AS created_at, COALESCE(ata.source, '') AS source
     FROM activity_taxonomy_assignments ata JOIN activities a ON a.id = ata.activity_id
     WHERE ata.taxonomy_id = '${SPENDING_TAXONOMY}' AND ${window}
     ORDER BY ata.activity_id, ata.created_at, ata.id;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 5 ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: parts[2], c3: parts[3], c4: spendTail(parts, 4) } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const splits = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending splits',
    `SELECT s.activity_id AS activity_id, s.category_id AS category_id, ROUND(ABS(CAST(COALESCE(s.amount, '0') AS REAL)) * 100) AS abs_cents
     FROM spending_activity_splits s JOIN activities a ON a.id = s.activity_id
     WHERE s.taxonomy_id = '${SPENDING_TAXONOMY}' AND ${window}
     ORDER BY s.activity_id, s.sort_order, s.id;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 3 ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: parts[2] } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const filingsOf = new Map<string, SpendRow['filings']>();
  for (const r of filings) {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    const list = filingsOf.get(String(v[0])) ?? [];
    list.push({ id: String(v[1] ?? ''), categoryId: String(v[2] ?? ''), createdAt: String(v[3] ?? ''), source: String(v[4] ?? '') });
    filingsOf.set(String(v[0]), list);
  }
  const splitsOf = new Map<string, SpendRow['splits']>();
  for (const r of splits) {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    const list = splitsOf.get(String(v[0])) ?? [];
    list.push({ categoryId: String(v[1] ?? ''), absCents: Math.round(Number(v[2]) || 0) });
    splitsOf.set(String(v[0]), list);
  }
  return activities.map((r): SpendRow => {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    const id = String(v[0]);
    const account = accountById.get(String(v[2] ?? ''));
    return {
      id, date: String(v[1] ?? ''), accountId: String(v[2] ?? ''), accountName: account?.name ?? '', accountType: account?.type ?? '',
      type: String(v[3] ?? ''), typeOverride: String(v[4] ?? ''), subtype: String(v[5] ?? ''), absCents: Math.round(Number(v[6]) || 0),
      groupId: String(v[7] ?? ''), notes: String(v[8] ?? ''), filings: filingsOf.get(id) ?? [], splits: splitsOf.get(id) ?? [],
    };
  });
}

/** Wealthfolio's Spending settings and its spending categories, as its page
 *  reads them (crates/spending settings/service.rs, insight/service.rs). */
export interface NativeSpendingSettings {
  /** Wealthfolio's timezone; null when unset. */
  timezone: string | null;
  /** `spending.excluded_category_ids`; [] when unset (3.8) or not a list of strings. */
  excludedCategoryIds: string[];
  /** Every spending category's parent (null at the top). */
  categoryParents: Record<string, string | null>;
  /** Every spending category's name. */
  categoryNames: Record<string, string>;
  /** Off unless `spending.enabled` is "true" and at least one account is opted in. */
  spendingOn: boolean;
  /** The accounts its Spending page reads: opted in, existing and not archived. */
  spendingAccountIds: string[];
}

/** Strict: throws NativeDbUnreadableError when the database cannot be read. */
export function getNativeSpendingSettings(dbPath: string): NativeSpendingSettings {
  const rows = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending settings',
    `SELECT setting_key AS k, COALESCE(setting_value, '') AS v FROM app_settings
     WHERE setting_key IN ('timezone', 'spending.excluded_category_ids', 'spending.enabled', 'spending.account_ids') ORDER BY setting_key;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 2 ? { c0: parts[0], c1: spendTail(parts, 1) } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const value = new Map(rows.map((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    return [String(v[0]), String(v[1] ?? '')];
  }));
  // As upstream reads a list setting: `Vec<String>` or nothing — one
  // non-string element, or text that is not JSON, is the whole list absent.
  const stringList = (key: string): string[] => {
    try {
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(value.get(key) || '[]');
      return Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.every((x) => typeof x === 'string') ? (parsed as string[]) : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  };
  const optedIn = value.get('spending.enabled') === 'true' ? stringList('spending.account_ids') : [];
  const accounts = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending settings accounts',
    `SELECT id AS id, COALESCE(is_archived, 0) AS archived FROM accounts ORDER BY id;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 2 ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1] } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const live = new Set(accounts.flatMap((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    return spendTrue(v[1]) ? [] : [String(v[0])];
  }));
  const categories = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'spending categories',
    `SELECT id AS id, COALESCE(parent_id, '') AS parent, COALESCE(name, '') AS name FROM taxonomy_categories
     WHERE taxonomy_id = '${SPENDING_TAXONOMY}' ORDER BY id;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 3 ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: spendTail(parts, 2) } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  const categoryParents: Record<string, string | null> = {};
  const categoryNames: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const r of categories) {
    const v = Object.values(r);
    categoryParents[String(v[0])] = String(v[1] ?? '') || null;
    categoryNames[String(v[0])] = String(v[2] ?? '');
  }
  return {
    timezone: value.get('timezone') || null,
    excludedCategoryIds: stringList('spending.excluded_category_ids'),
    categoryParents,
    categoryNames,
    spendingOn: optedIn.length > 0,
    spendingAccountIds: optedIn.filter((id) => live.has(id)),
  };
}

const shiftDay = (day: string, days: number) => new Date(Date.parse(`${day}T00:00:00Z`) + days * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

/** The lines Wealthfolio's Spending page counts on its local days
 *  `[startDay, endDayExclusive)`, and the settings they were counted with.
 *  Rows are read a day wider on each side: a row's local date is at most a
 *  day from its UTC date. Strict. */
export function readSpendLines(dbPath: string, startDay: string, endDayExclusive: string): { lines: SpendLine[]; settings: NativeSpendingSettings } {
  if (!isSpendDay(startDay) || !isSpendDay(endDayExclusive)) throw new Error(`malformed spending bounds: ${startDay}..${endDayExclusive}`);
  const settings = getNativeSpendingSettings(dbPath);
  const rows = getNativeSpendRows(dbPath, shiftDay(startDay, -1), shiftDay(endDayExclusive, 1));
  const lines = spendLines(rows, {
    timezone: settings.timezone,
    spendingAccountIds: settings.spendingAccountIds,
    excludedCategoryIds: settings.excludedCategoryIds,
    categoryParents: settings.categoryParents,
  }).filter((l) => l.day >= startDay && l.day < endDayExclusive);
  return { lines, settings };
}

/** A category's name rolled up to its top-level ancestor, as every report
 *  labels spending; '' when the category is unknown. */
export function topCategoryName(categoryId: string, settings: Pick<NativeSpendingSettings, 'categoryParents' | 'categoryNames'>): string {
  return settings.categoryNames[topCategoryId(categoryId, settings.categoryParents)] ?? '';
}

```

```ts
}

const CARD_SPEND = new Set(['WITHDRAWAL', 'FEE', 'INTEREST']);
const CASH_SPEND = new Set(['WITHDRAWAL', 'TRANSFER_OUT', 'FEE', 'TAX']);
const REFUND_SUBTYPES = new Set(['REFUND', 'REBATE', 'REIMBURSEMENT']);

/** The report's spending sign — kept in step with `SPENDING_SIGN` in
 *  companion/src/sqlite-native.ts, which transcribes Wealthfolio's classifier. */
export function reportSpendSign(r: Pick<HeadlineDbRow, 'accountType' | 'type' | 'subtype'>): -1 | 0 | 1 {
  if (r.accountType === 'CREDIT_CARD') return CARD_SPEND.has(r.type) ? 1 : r.type === 'CREDIT' ? -1 : 0;
  if (r.accountType === 'CASH') {
    if (CASH_SPEND.has(r.type)) return 1;
    return r.type === 'CREDIT' && REFUND_SUBTYPES.has(r.subtype) ? -1 : 0;
  }
  return 0;
}

/** What the report drops by its marker (SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_EXCLUSION): the
 *  sync's corrections and in-transit placeholders — not starting balances. */
const reportDropsByMarker = (notes: string) => isCorrectionNote(notes) || isPlaceholderNote(notes);
const TRANSFER_TYPES = new Set(['TRANSFER_IN', 'TRANSFER_OUT']);

/** Stored at exactly 00:00 UTC — the only midnight both sides read differently. */
const atUtcMidnight = (date: string) => /T00:00:00(?:\.0+)?(?:Z|\+00:00)?$/.test(date);

/**
 * The excluded categories and every descendant, as Wealthfolio's
 * ExclusionIndex builds them: the raw ids are kept even when the category no
 * longer exists, and the walk up each parent chain is bounded (32 steps), so
 * a cyclic taxonomy cannot hang it.
 */
export function excludedWithDescendants(raw: readonly string[], parents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>): Set<string> {
  const excluded = new Set(raw);
  if (excluded.size === 0) return excluded;
  const roots = new Set(raw);
  for (const id of Object.keys(parents)) {
    if (roots.has(id)) continue;
    const seen = new Set([id]);
    let current = id;
    for (let depth = 0; depth < 32; depth++) {
      const parent = parents[current];
      if (!parent || seen.has(parent)) break;
      if (roots.has(parent)) {
        excluded.add(id);
        break;
      }
      seen.add(parent);
      current = parent;
    }
  }
  return excluded;
}

export function explainHeadline(facts: HeadlineFacts): HeadlineExplanation {
  const failed = new Set(facts.failed ?? []);
```

```ts
    const add = (kind: HeadlineReasonKind, cents: number, row: HeadlineRow) => {
      const r = byKind.get(kind)!;
      r.cents += cents;
      r.rows.push(row);
    };
    const tracked = facts.spendingAccountIds ? new Set(facts.spendingAccountIds) : null;
    const covered = facts.reportAccountIds ? new Set(facts.reportAccountIds) : null;

    const wfEnd = Date.parse(bounds.end);

          : r.categoryIds.length === 0 && r.groupId === '' && spend > 0 ? 1 : 0;
      const row: HeadlineRow = { date: r.date.slice(0, 10), description: descriptionFromComment(r.notes), cents: spend, account: r.accountName };
      // Excluded: Wealthfolio counts it in no month at all.
      if (excluded && r.spendingCategoryId && excluded.has(r.spendingCategoryId)) {
        if (filings > 0) {
          add('excluded', spend, row);
          if (filings > 1) add('double-filed', spend * (filings - 1), row);
        }
        continue;
      }
      const at = Date.parse(r.date);
      if (!Number.isNaN(at) && (at < wfStart || at > wfEnd)) {
        // Wealthfolio counts it in another month; only this one is named.
        if (filings > 0 && r.date.startsWith(firstAtMidnight) && atUtcMidnight(r.date)) {
          add('first-midnight', spend, row);
          if (filings > 1) add('double-filed', spend * (filings - 1), row);
        }
        continue;
      }
      // Wealthfolio counts it, once.
      if (filings > 1) add('double-filed', spend * (filings - 1), row);
      if (filings > 0) continue;
```

---

### Task 3: Every report figure counts spending as Wealthfolio does

Ruling W4 for every reader (Q1d). Each spending reader in `sqlite-native.ts` adds up `readSpendLines`: categories by top-level name from the lines' allocations (`getNativeWealthfolioSpendingBetween`, `…TopSpending`, `…SubcategorySpending`, `getNativeSpendMatrix`, `getNativeDrillRows`), Uncategorized from `uncategorizedCents`, refunds netted (`getNativeUncategorizedSpendingTotal`, `…ByMonthAccount`), every line (`getNativeMerchantRows`, `getNativeSpendDailyTotals`); `getNativeBudgetSpend` is the matrix, strict, with no scope argument. The lenient readers still answer empty for a database that is missing or unreadable; the strict ones still throw. Budget vs actual (`ledger-facts.ts`) reads the same lines. The dismissed-id arguments go from the readers, the pool's and the cube's dependencies and `index.ts`'s bindings; the dead SQL pieces and the drill CLI parser go. Fixtures gain the real schema's columns (`activity_type_override`, an assignment's id, `created_at` and default spending taxonomy, `spending_activity_splits`, `is_archived`) and Wealthfolio's timezone (`UTC`, so a stored date is the day it counts on wherever the suite runs). Every changed expectation is one of the decided deviations: a dismissed charge, a charge awaiting its transfer's other half and a transfer-named category now count; a placeholder a rule filed counts as Wealthfolio counts it, and one filed under an excluded "Sync bookkeeping" counts nowhere. The agreement matrix runs again through the real readers on a real database, and the real-sqlite3 test drives the new reader through the CLI.

- Create: `companion/src/wf-spending-agreement.test.ts`
- Modify: `companion/src/sqlite-native.ts`, `companion/src/ledger-facts.ts`, `companion/src/pool-report.ts`, `companion/src/report-cube-build.ts`, `companion/src/budget-suggest-host.ts`, `companion/src/index.ts`, `shared/report-cube.ts`
- Modify (tests): `companion/src/sqlite-native.test.ts`, `companion/src/sqlite-native-real-cli.test.ts`, `companion/src/ledger-facts.test.ts`, `companion/src/pool-report.test.ts`, `companion/src/report-cube-build.test.ts`

- Produces:

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `companion/src/wf-spending-agreement.test.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * The report and Wealthfolio's Spending page agree (1.62, ruling W4), checked
 * through the real readers on a real database: for each rule 1.61's audit
 * named a reason for, the digest's month total (categories + uncategorized)
 * and the cube's (matrix + uncategorized cells) equal the figure Wealthfolio's
 * own rules give — worked out by hand from upstream crates/spending — and the
 * audit names no reason.
 */
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { explainHeadline } from '../../shared/audit-headline.js';
import {
  getNativeSpendingSettings, getNativeSpendMatrix, getNativeUncategorizedByMonthAccount, getNativeUncategorizedSpendingTotal,
  getNativeWealthfolioSpending, readSpendLines,
} from './sqlite-native.js';
import { getNativeHeadlineRows } from './sqlite-audit.js';

const made: string[] = [];
afterEach(() => { for (const d of made.splice(0)) rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

/** Wealthfolio's real tables, New York time, two spending accounts and savings. */
function database(rows: string, settings = ''): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'sfin-agree-'));
  made.push(dir);
  const path = join(dir, 'wealthfolio.db');
  const db = new DatabaseSync(path);
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE accounts (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, account_type TEXT, is_archived INTEGER DEFAULT 0);
    CREATE TABLE activities (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, account_id TEXT, activity_type TEXT, activity_type_override TEXT, subtype TEXT,
      activity_date TEXT, amount TEXT, notes TEXT, source_group_id TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE taxonomy_categories (id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT, name TEXT, parent_id TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE activity_taxonomy_assignments (id TEXT, activity_id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT, category_id TEXT, source TEXT, created_at TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE spending_activity_splits (id TEXT, activity_id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT, category_id TEXT, amount TEXT, sort_order INTEGER);
    CREATE TABLE app_settings (setting_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, setting_value TEXT);
    INSERT INTO accounts VALUES ('chk', 'PNC Checking', 'CASH', 0), ('visa', 'Visa', 'CREDIT_CARD', 0), ('sav', 'Ally Savings', 'CASH', 0);
    INSERT INTO app_settings VALUES ('timezone', 'America/New_York'), ('spending.enabled', 'true'), ('spending.account_ids', '["chk","visa"]');
    INSERT INTO taxonomy_categories VALUES
      ('food', 'spending_categories', 'Food & Dining', NULL), ('rest', 'spending_categories', 'Restaurants', 'food'),
      ('edu', 'spending_categories', 'Education', NULL), ('tuition', 'spending_categories', 'Tuition', 'edu'),
      ('home', 'spending_categories', 'Home', NULL), ('sync', 'spending_categories', 'Sync bookkeeping', NULL);
    ${settings}
    ${rows}
  `);
  db.close();
  return path;
}

/** The digest's "spent this month" and the cube's, in cents. */
function reportCents(path: string): { digest: number; cube: number } {
  const categories = Object.values(getNativeWealthfolioSpending(path, '2026-10')).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
  const digest = Math.round((categories + getNativeUncategorizedSpendingTotal(path, '2026-10-01', '2026-11-01').total) * 100);
  const matrix = getNativeSpendMatrix(path, '2026-10-01', '2026-11-01').filter((r) => r.month === '2026-10').reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  const uncat = getNativeUncategorizedByMonthAccount(path, '2026-10-01', '2026-11-01').filter((r) => r.month === '2026-10').reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  return { digest, cube: Math.round((matrix + uncat) * 100) };
}

const filed = (activity: string, category: string, at = '2026-10-05T00:00:00Z', id = `${activity}-${category}`) =>
  `INSERT INTO activity_taxonomy_assignments VALUES ('${id}', '${activity}', 'spending_categories', '${category}', 'manual', '${at}');`;

describe('the report counts each case exactly as Wealthfolio does', () => {
  it.each<[string, string, string, number]>([
    ['an excluded category and its descendant', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '15', 'TUITION · TRN-1', NULL);
      ${filed('a1', 'tuition')}
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '100', 'KROGER · TRN-2', NULL);
      ${filed('a2', 'food')}`, `INSERT INTO app_settings VALUES ('spending.excluded_category_ids', '["edu"]');`, 10000],
    ['a row filed under two categories counts once', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'visa', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '12', 'SPLIT RENT · TRN-1', NULL);
      ${filed('a1', 'home', '2026-10-06T00:00:00Z')}
      ${filed('a1', 'rest', '2026-10-05T00:00:00Z')}`, '', 1200],
    ['an uncategorized refund is netted', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '50', 'TARGET · TRN-1', NULL);
      ${filed('a1', 'home')}
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'chk', 'CREDIT', NULL, 'REFUND', '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '8', 'RETURN · TRN-2', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a3', 'visa', 'CREDIT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-07T12:00:00+00:00', '3', 'STATEMENT CREDIT · TRN-3', NULL);`, '', 3900],
    ['a row stored at 00:00 UTC on the 1st is September’s in New York', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', '25', 'RENT PORTAL · TRN-1', NULL);
      ${filed('a1', 'home')}
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00', '10', 'COFFEE · TRN-2', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a3', 'visa', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-11-01T02:00:00+00:00', '40', 'HALLOWEEN · TRN-3', NULL);
      ${filed('a3', 'rest')}`, '', 5000],
    ['a linked transfer out to savings is a saving, not spending', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '500', 'TO ALLY · TRN-1', 'g-sav');
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'sav', 'TRANSFER_IN', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '500', 'FROM PNC · TRN-2', 'g-sav');
      ${filed('a1', 'home')}
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a3', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '20', 'LUNCH · TRN-3', NULL);
      ${filed('a3', 'rest')}`, '', 2000],
    ['an activity type override is the type that counts', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'DEPOSIT', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '30', 'MISTYPED · TRN-1', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', 'DEPOSIT', NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '70', 'REFUNDED · TRN-2', NULL);
      ${filed('a2', 'home')}`, '', 3000],
    ['the sync’s bookkeeping rows filed under its excluded category (3.9+)', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '1000', '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-1', NULL);
      ${filed('a1', 'sync')}
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '20', 'LUNCH · TRN-2', NULL);`,
      `INSERT INTO app_settings VALUES ('spending.excluded_category_ids', '["sync"]');`, 2000],
    ['the sync’s bookkeeping rows on 3.8, which excludes nothing', `
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '6', 'Balance adjustment · sfin-chk · 2026-10-05', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a2', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '20', 'LUNCH · TRN-2', NULL);`, '', 2600],
  ])('%s', (_name, rows, settings, wealthfolio) => {
    const path = database(rows, settings);
    const report = reportCents(path);
    expect(report).toEqual({ digest: wealthfolio, cube: wealthfolio });
    const s = getNativeSpendingSettings(path);
    const h = explainHeadline({
      month: '2026-10', timezone: s.timezone, spendingAccountIds: s.spendingAccountIds, reportAccountIds: ['chk', 'visa'],
      excludedCategoryIds: s.excludedCategoryIds, categoryParents: s.categoryParents,
      reportCents: report.cube, wealthfolioCents: wealthfolio, rows: getNativeHeadlineRows(path, '2026-10-01', '2026-11-01'),
    });
    expect(h.reasons).toEqual([]);
    expect(h.unexplainedCents).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('readSpendLines and the settings behind it', () => {
  it('names every spending category and reads the Spending page’s accounts, archived ones out', () => {
    const path = database(`UPDATE accounts SET is_archived = 1 WHERE id = 'visa';`);
    expect(getNativeSpendingSettings(path)).toMatchObject({
      timezone: 'America/New_York', spendingOn: true, spendingAccountIds: ['chk'],
      categoryNames: { food: 'Food & Dining', rest: 'Restaurants' }, categoryParents: { rest: 'food', food: null },
    });
  });

  it('places rows on Wealthfolio’s local days, reading a day wider on each side', () => {
    const path = database(`
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('sept', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-01T03:59:59+00:00', '1', 'A · TRN-1', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('first', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00', '2', 'B · TRN-2', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('last', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-11-01T03:59:59+00:00', '4', 'C · TRN-3', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('nov', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-11-01T04:00:00+00:00', '8', 'D · TRN-4', NULL);`);
    const { lines } = readSpendLines(path, '2026-10-01', '2026-11-01');
    expect(lines.map((l) => [l.row.id, l.day])).toEqual([['first', '2026-10-01'], ['last', '2026-10-31']]);
  });

  it('counts nothing while Wealthfolio’s Spending page is off, as Wealthfolio counts nothing', () => {
    const path = database(`
      UPDATE app_settings SET setting_value = 'false' WHERE setting_key = 'spending.enabled';
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('a1', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '20', 'LUNCH · TRN-1', NULL);`);
    expect(readSpendLines(path, '2026-10-01', '2026-11-01').lines).toEqual([]);
    expect(reportCents(path)).toEqual({ digest: 0, cube: 0 });
  });
});
```

```ts
    -- the fixture carries the SQLite name deliberately.
    CREATE TABLE activities (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, amount TEXT, activity_date TEXT, activity_type TEXT, notes TEXT, account_id TEXT DEFAULT 'acct-cash', subtype TEXT, currency TEXT, source_group_id TEXT, activity_type_override TEXT);
    -- A filing's taxonomy defaults to spending, like the categories': the
    -- spending readers read the assignment's own taxonomy, as Wealthfolio does.
    CREATE TABLE activity_taxonomy_assignments (activity_id TEXT, category_id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT DEFAULT 'spending_categories', source TEXT, id TEXT, created_at TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE spending_activity_splits (id TEXT, activity_id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT, category_id TEXT, amount TEXT, sort_order INTEGER);
    CREATE TABLE budget_targets (category_id TEXT, amount TEXT, period_key TEXT, updated_at TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE accounts (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, account_type TEXT, is_archived INTEGER DEFAULT 0);
```

```ts
describe('parseDrillCliLine (the sqlite3 CLI fallback’s row parser)', () => {
  const U = '\x1f';
  const line = (...cols: string[]) => cols.join(U);
  it('reads a full line, keeping a pipe in notes intact', () => {
    expect(parseDrillCliLine(line('Dining', '2026-09-03', 'Card', '9.5', 'p1', 'acct-card', 'cat-d', 'rule', 'WITHDRAWAL', 'CAFE | BAR · TRN-9'))).toEqual({
      c0: 'Dining', c1: '2026-09-03', c2: 'Card', c3: 9.5, c4: 'p1', c5: 'acct-card', c6: 'cat-d', c7: 'rule', c8: 'WITHDRAWAL', c9: 'CAFE | BAR · TRN-9',
    });
  });
  it('keeps the fields aligned when a category and an account name contain a pipe', () => {
    expect(parseDrillCliLine(line('Food|Drink', '2026-09-03', 'Joe|s Visa', '9', 'p1', 'acct-pipe', 'cat-pipe', 'rule', 'WITHDRAWAL', 'N'))).toMatchObject({
      c0: 'Food|Drink', c2: 'Joe|s Visa', c4: 'p1', c5: 'acct-pipe', c6: 'cat-pipe', c7: 'rule', c9: 'N',
    });
  });
  it('drops a blank, short or shifted line', () => {
    expect(parseDrillCliLine('   ')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseDrillCliLine(line('Dining', '2026-09-03', 'Card', '9'))).toBeNull();
    expect(parseDrillCliLine(line('Dining', '2026-09-03', 'Card', 'not-a-number', 'p1', 'a', 'c', 's', 'T', 'N'))).toBeNull();
  });
```

```ts
function spentCentsBetween(
  deps: PoolReportDeps,
  startInclusive: string,
  endExclusive: string,
  dismissedIds: string[],

    .reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0);
  const uncategorized = deps.uncategorizedTotal(startInclusive, endExclusive, dismissedIds).total;

}
```

```ts
/**
 * The joins and predicates that define "spending", shared verbatim by the
 * aggregate reader and the biggest-transactions reader so the two can never
 * disagree about what counts. In particular the transfers exclusion has to apply
 * to both: a transfer miscategorised as spending is typically the largest row of
 * the week and would otherwise top the list.
 */
const SPENDING_FROM = `
    FROM activities a
    JOIN accounts acc ON a.account_id = acc.id
    JOIN activity_taxonomy_assignments ata ON a.id = ata.activity_id
    JOIN taxonomy_categories tc ON ata.category_id = tc.id
    LEFT JOIN taxonomy_categories parent ON tc.parent_id = parent.id`;

/** The rolled-up label: the parent category where there is one, else the
 *  category itself. Both readers select and filter on this same expression. */
const SPENDING_CATEGORY = `COALESCE(parent.name, tc.name)`;

/** A spending filing every spending reader counts: its rolled-up category is
 *  not named like a transfer. Over `tc` (the category) and `parent` (its
 *  parent, LEFT JOINed). Exported for the audits' month read
 *  (sqlite-audit.ts), which counts the report's filings per row. */
export const REPORT_COUNTS_CATEGORY = `LOWER(${SPENDING_CATEGORY}) NOT IN ('transfers', 'transfer', 'internal transfers', 'savings & transfers')`;

/**
 * Wealthfolio's own spending classification, transcribed from upstream —
 * `docs/upstream-spending-buckets.md` §2 quotes the Rust with line numbers.
 * `+1` is an Expense, `-1` an ExpenseRefund (money BACK, which reduces what a
```

```ts
const SPENDING_SIGNED_AMOUNT = `(${SPENDING_SIGN}) * ABS(CAST(a.amount AS REAL))`;

 * neutral there, and 3.8 left no cash-moving outflow type that isn't). So the
 * type cannot tell a placeholder from a real transfer, a Wealthfolio rule can
 * even auto-file one before its counterpart posts — and the marker is the one
 * thing that is unambiguously the sync's. The single quote is the only
 * character SQL cares about in these prefixes; neither contains one.
 */

      AND COALESCE(a.notes, '') NOT LIKE '${BALANCE_ADJUSTMENT_COMMENT_PREFIX}%'
```

```ts
function spendingWhere(startInclusive: string, endExclusive: string): string {
  return `
    WHERE a.activity_date >= '${startInclusive}'
      AND a.activity_date < '${endExclusive}'
      AND (${SPENDING_SIGN}) <> 0
      ${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_EXCLUSION}
      -- The SPENDING taxonomy only. A reimbursement carries TWO assignments —
      -- an income one and a spending one — so joining every taxonomy counted
      -- the same row twice, under two different category names.
      AND tc.taxonomy_id = 'spending_categories'
      AND ${REPORT_COUNTS_CATEGORY}`;
}

/**
```
```ts
 * The spending lines the lenient readers below add up (1.62): Wealthfolio's
 * own definition (shared/wf-spending.ts), on its local days. Null — never
 * "nothing spent" — when the database is missing, the bounds are malformed or
 * a read failed; those readers then answer empty, as they always have.
 */
function spendLinesOrNull(dbPath: string, startInclusive: string, endExclusive: string): ReturnType<typeof readSpendLines> | null {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath) || !validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) return null;
  try {
    return readSpendLines(dbPath, startInclusive, endExclusive);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[simplefin-sync] Failed to read spending:', err);
    return null;
  }
}
```

```ts
): Record<string, number> {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) {
    return {};
  }
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) {
    return {};
  }

  const query = `
    SELECT
      ${SPENDING_CATEGORY} as parent_category,
      ROUND(SUM(${SPENDING_SIGNED_AMOUNT}), 2) as total_spent
    ${SPENDING_FROM}
    ${spendingWhere(startInclusive, endExclusive)}
    GROUP BY ${SPENDING_CATEGORY};
  `;

  const rows = queryNativeDb<{ parent_category: string; total_spent: number }>(
    dbPath,
    'spending',
    query,
    (parts) => (parts.length === 2
      ? { parent_category: parts[0].trim(), total_spent: parseFloat(parts[1]) || 0 }
      : null),
  );

  const result: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.parent_category) {
      result[r.parent_category] = typeof r.total_spent === 'number' ? r.total_spent : parseFloat(String(r.total_spent || 0));
    }
  }
  return result;
}
```

```ts
): NativeSpendingTransaction[] {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) {
    return [];
  }
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) {
    return [];
  }
  const n = Number.isFinite(limit) ? Math.floor(limit) : 0;
  if (n < 1) return [];

  const query = `
    SELECT
      ROUND(ABS(CAST(a.amount AS REAL)), 2) as amount,
      COALESCE(a.notes, '') as notes,
      ${SPENDING_CATEGORY} as parent_category
    ${SPENDING_FROM}
    ${spendingWhere(startInclusive, endExclusive)}
      -- EXPENSES only, unlike the aggregate readers. "Biggest spends this week"
      -- is a list of purchases; a large refund is money coming back and would
      -- head the list as though it were the week's worst damage.
      AND (${SPENDING_SIGN}) > 0
    ORDER BY ABS(CAST(a.amount AS REAL)) DESC
    LIMIT ${n};
  `;

  // The CLI fallback splits on `|`, which a bank description can legitimately
  // contain, so the DESCRIPTION is read as "everything between the first and
  // last field" rather than by index. (A `|` in a category name would still
  // confuse it; the aggregate reader has the same exposure, and the node:sqlite
  // path — the one that actually runs — is unaffected.)
  const rows = queryNativeDb<{ amount: number | string; notes: string; parent_category: string }>(
    dbPath,
    'top spending',
    query,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 3
      ? {
          amount: parseFloat(parts[0]) || 0,
          notes: parts.slice(1, -1).join('|'),
          parent_category: parts[parts.length - 1].trim(),
        }
      : null),
  );

  return rows.map((r) => ({
    amount: typeof r.amount === 'number' ? r.amount : parseFloat(String(r.amount || 0)),
    description: descriptionFromComment(r.notes),
    categoryName: r.parent_category,
  }));
```

```ts
): NativeSpendingTransaction[] {
  const n = Number.isFinite(limit) ? Math.floor(limit) : 0;
  if (n < 1) return [];
  const read = spendLinesOrNull(dbPath, startInclusive, endExclusive);
  if (!read) return [];
  // EXPENSES only, per category it counts under. "Biggest spends this week"
  // is a list of purchases; a large refund is money coming back and would
  // head the list as though it were the week's worst damage.
  const spends: Array<{ cents: number; notes: string; categoryName: string }> = [];
  for (const line of read.lines) {
    for (const a of line.allocations) {
      const categoryName = topCategoryName(a.categoryId, read.settings);
      if (a.cents > 0 && categoryName) spends.push({ cents: a.cents, notes: line.row.notes, categoryName });
    }
  }
  return spends.sort((x, y) => y.cents - x.cents).slice(0, n).map((s) => ({
    amount: toDollars(s.cents),
    description: descriptionFromComment(s.notes),
    categoryName: s.categoryName,
  }));
```

```ts
): NativeSubcategorySpend[] {
  const read = spendLinesOrNull(dbPath, startInclusive, endExclusive);
  if (!read) return [];
  const byKey = new Map<string, { parent: string; child: string | null; cents: number }>();
  for (const line of read.lines) {
    for (const a of line.allocations) {
      const top = topCategoryId(a.categoryId, read.settings.categoryParents);
      const parent = read.settings.categoryNames[top] ?? '';
      if (!parent) continue;
      const child = top === a.categoryId ? null : read.settings.categoryNames[a.categoryId] || null;
      const key = `${parent}\u0000${child ?? ''}`;
      const entry = byKey.get(key) ?? { parent, child, cents: 0 };
      entry.cents += a.cents;
      byKey.set(key, entry);
    }
  }
  return [...byKey.values()]
    .sort((x, y) => (x.parent === y.parent ? (x.child ?? '').localeCompare(y.child ?? '') : x.parent.localeCompare(y.parent)))
    .map((e) => ({ parent: e.parent, child: e.child, spent: toDollars(e.cents) }));
}
```

```ts
  endExclusive: string,
  /**
   * Activity IDs the user has dismissed from the "needs a category" list.
   *
   * Dismissing says "this is not spending I need to file" — but the total fed
   * the daily digest's pool regardless, so a dismissed charge went on
   * suppressing every category's figure with no line anywhere admitting why.
   * Excluded here rather than at the call site so the count and the total can
   * never disagree about which rows they describe.
   */
  dismissedActivityIds: readonly string[] = [],
): { count: number; total: number } {
  const empty = { count: 0, total: 0 };
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) return empty;
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) return empty;

  // Ids come from an addon secret, so they are quoted defensively rather than
  // interpolated bare — and anything not matching an activity-id shape is
  // dropped instead of escaped, since a malformed id can only be junk.
  const safeIds = dismissedActivityIds.filter((id) => /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(id));
  const dismissedClause = safeIds.length
    ? `AND a.id NOT IN (${safeIds.map((id) => `'${id}'`).join(',')})`
    : '';

  const query = `
    SELECT COUNT(*) as n,
           ROUND(SUM(ABS(CAST(a.amount AS REAL))), 2) as total
    FROM activities a
    JOIN accounts acc ON a.account_id = acc.id
    LEFT JOIN activity_taxonomy_assignments ata ON a.id = ata.activity_id
    WHERE a.activity_date >= '${startInclusive}'
      AND a.activity_date < '${endExclusive}'
      AND ata.activity_id IS NULL
      AND COALESCE(a.source_group_id, '') = ''
      ${dismissedClause}
      ${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_EXCLUSION}
      ${IN_SPENDING_SCOPE}
      ${NOT_AWAITING_TRANSFER_HALF}
      AND (${SPENDING_SIGN}) > 0;
  `;

  const rows = queryNativeDb<{ n: number | string; total: number | string | null }>(
    dbPath,
    'uncategorized total',
    query,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 2 ? { n: parts[0], total: parts[1] } : null),
  );
  const row = rows[0];
  if (!row) return empty;
  const count = typeof row.n === 'number' ? row.n : parseInt(String(row.n), 10) || 0;
  const total = typeof row.total === 'number' ? row.total : parseFloat(String(row.total ?? 0)) || 0;
  return { count, total };
}
```

with:

```ts
/**
 * Signed spend per (month, top-level category, account) — the report cube's
 * central series, from the same lines as every other spending reader
 * (shared/wf-spending.ts), so the Budget tab can never disagree with the
 * digest, or with Wealthfolio, about what a month cost. The month is
 * Wealthfolio's local one.
 */
function spendMatrixOf(read: ReturnType<typeof readSpendLines>): Array<{ month: string; category: string; accountId: string; amount: number }> {
  const byKey = new Map<string, { month: string; category: string; accountId: string; cents: number }>();
  for (const line of read.lines) {
    for (const a of line.allocations) {
      const category = topCategoryName(a.categoryId, read.settings);
      if (!category) continue;
      const key = `${line.month}\u0000${category}\u0000${line.row.accountId}`;
      const entry = byKey.get(key) ?? { month: line.month, category, accountId: line.row.accountId, cents: 0 };
      entry.cents += a.cents;
      byKey.set(key, entry);
    }
  }
  return [...byKey.values()].map((e) => ({ month: e.month, category: e.category, accountId: e.accountId, amount: toDollars(e.cents) }));
}
```

```ts
): Array<{ month: string; category: string; accountId: string; amount: number }> {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) return [];
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) return [];
  const query = `
    SELECT strftime('%Y-%m', a.activity_date) as month,
           ${SPENDING_CATEGORY} as category,
           COALESCE(a.account_id, '') as account_id,
           ROUND(SUM(${SPENDING_SIGNED_AMOUNT}), 2) as amount
    ${SPENDING_FROM}
    ${spendingWhere(startInclusive, endExclusive)}
    GROUP BY month, category, account_id;
  `;
  const rows = queryNativeDb<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath,
    'spend matrix',
    query,
    (parts) => (parts.length === 4
      ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: parts[2], c3: parseFloat(parts[3]) || 0 }
      : null),
  );
  return rows.map((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r) as [string, string, string, number | string];
    return {
      month: String(v[0]),
      category: String(v[1]),
      accountId: String(v[2]),
      amount: typeof v[3] === 'number' ? v[3] : parseFloat(String(v[3])) || 0,
    };
  }).filter((r) => r.month && r.category);

 * Suggested budgets' spend read (project 4): `getNativeSpendMatrix`'s series,
 * restricted to what Wealthfolio's budget actuals count in two respects. It is

 * under a category counts, `activity_type_override` and
 * `spending_activity_splits` are ignored).
 *  - Only the SPENDING accounts (`getNativeAuditSettings.spendingAccountIds`):
 *    Wealthfolio's budgets never see the others.
 *  - A row whose OWN category is excluded (`spending.excluded_category_ids`,
 *    descendants included) is dropped BEFORE it rolls up, so an excluded
 *    subcategory's spending never lands in its parent's figure.
 * The matrix is grouped by the row's own category id so both can be applied;
 * the account and category ids are filtered here, in code, never interpolated
 * into SQL (only the validated date bounds are). Strict: a database that
 * cannot be read throws `NativeDbUnreadableError` — never "nothing spent".
```

```ts
 * a database that cannot be read throws `NativeDbUnreadableError` — never
 * "nothing spent".
 */
```

```ts
  endExclusive: string,
  scope: { spendingAccountIds: readonly string[]; excludedCategoryIds: readonly string[]; categoryParents: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>> },
): Array<{ month: string; category: string; accountId: string; amount: number }> {
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) throw new Error('Refusing the budget spend read: malformed date bounds');
  const query = `
    SELECT strftime('%Y-%m', a.activity_date) as month,
           ${SPENDING_CATEGORY} as category,
           tc.id as category_id,
           COALESCE(a.account_id, '') as account_id,
           ROUND(SUM(${SPENDING_SIGNED_AMOUNT}), 2) as amount
    ${SPENDING_FROM}
    ${spendingWhere(startInclusive, endExclusive)}
    GROUP BY month, category, category_id, account_id;
  `;
  const rows = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath,
    'budget spend',
    query,
    (parts) => (parts.length === 5
      ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: parts[2], c3: parts[3], c4: parseFloat(parts[4]) || 0 }
      : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR,
  );
  const accounts = new Set(scope.spendingAccountIds);
  const dropped = excludedWithDescendants(scope.excludedCategoryIds, scope.categoryParents);
  const cents = new Map<string, { month: string; category: string; accountId: string; cents: number }>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    const [month, category, categoryId, accountId, amount] = Object.values(r) as [string, string, string, string, number | string];
    if (!month || !category || !accounts.has(String(accountId)) || dropped.has(String(categoryId))) continue;
    const key = `${month}\u0000${category}\u0000${accountId}`;
    const entry = cents.get(key) ?? { month: String(month), category: String(category), accountId: String(accountId), cents: 0 };
    entry.cents += Math.round((typeof amount === 'number' ? amount : parseFloat(String(amount)) || 0) * 100);
    cents.set(key, entry);
  }
  return [...cents.values()].map((e) => ({ month: e.month, category: e.category, accountId: e.accountId, amount: e.cents / 100 }));
}
```

```ts
  endExclusive: string,
  excludedActivityIds: readonly string[] = [],
): Array<{ month: string; accountId: string; amount: number }> {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) return [];
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) return [];
  const safeIds = excludedActivityIds.filter((id) => /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(id));
  const excludedClause = safeIds.length
    ? `AND a.id NOT IN (${safeIds.map((id) => `'${id}'`).join(',')})`
    : '';
  const query = `
    SELECT strftime('%Y-%m', a.activity_date) as month,
           COALESCE(a.account_id, '') as account_id,
           ROUND(SUM(ABS(CAST(a.amount AS REAL))), 2) as amount
    FROM activities a
    JOIN accounts acc ON a.account_id = acc.id
    LEFT JOIN activity_taxonomy_assignments ata ON a.id = ata.activity_id
    WHERE a.activity_date >= '${startInclusive}'
      AND a.activity_date < '${endExclusive}'
      AND ata.activity_id IS NULL
      AND COALESCE(a.source_group_id, '') = ''
      ${excludedClause}
      ${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_EXCLUSION}
      ${IN_SPENDING_SCOPE}
      ${NOT_AWAITING_TRANSFER_HALF}
      AND (${SPENDING_SIGN}) > 0
    GROUP BY month, account_id;
  `;
  const rows = queryNativeDb<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath,
    'uncategorized by month',
    query,
    (parts) => (parts.length === 3
      ? { c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: parseFloat(parts[2]) || 0 }
      : null),
  );
  return rows.map((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r) as [string, string, number | string];
    return {
      month: String(v[0]),
      accountId: String(v[1]),
      amount: typeof v[2] === 'number' ? v[2] : parseFloat(String(v[2])) || 0,
    };
  }).filter((r) => r.month);
}
```

```ts
export const DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR = '\x1f';

/** Rebuild a drill row from one split CLI line (ten columns, notes last), or
 *  null for a malformed or shifted line. */
export function drillRowFromCliParts(parts: string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (parts.length < 10 || !Number.isFinite(parseFloat(parts[3]))) return null;
  return {
    c0: parts[0], c1: parts[1], c2: parts[2], c3: parseFloat(parts[3]) || 0, c4: parts[4], c5: parts[5],
    c6: parts[6], c7: parts[7], c8: parts[8], c9: parts.slice(9).join(DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR),
  };
}

/** One line of the CLI fallback's output, as `queryNativeDb` reads it. */
export function parseDrillCliLine(line: string): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  const trimmed = line.trim();
  return trimmed ? drillRowFromCliParts(trimmed.split(DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR)) : null;
}

/**

 * definition the spend matrix uses, so a category's list always sums to the
 * bar the user clicked. Newest first; the cube builder caps per category.
 */
```

```ts
): NativeDrillRow[] {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) return [];
  if (!validDateBounds(startInclusive, endExclusive)) return [];
  // Names come back unchanged on the node:sqlite path (they must match the
  // cube's other matrices). The CLI fallback uses the unit separator, so a `|`
  // in any column cannot shift the others; notes stay last anyway.
  const query = `
    SELECT ${SPENDING_CATEGORY} as category,
           date(a.activity_date) as date,
           COALESCE(acc.name, '') as account,
           ROUND(${SPENDING_SIGNED_AMOUNT}, 2) as amount,
           a.id as activity_id,
           COALESCE(a.account_id, '') as account_id,
           COALESCE(ata.category_id, '') as category_id,
           COALESCE(ata.source, '') as source,
           UPPER(COALESCE(a.activity_type, '')) as activity_type,
           COALESCE(a.notes, '') as notes
    ${SPENDING_FROM}
    ${spendingWhere(startInclusive, endExclusive)}
    ORDER BY a.activity_date DESC, a.id;
  `;
  const rows = queryNativeDb<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath,
    'drill rows',
    query,
    drillRowFromCliParts,
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR,
  );
  return rows.map((r) => {
    const v = Object.values(r) as Array<string | number>;
    return {
      category: String(v[0]),
      date: String(v[1]),
      account: String(v[2]),
      amount: typeof v[3] === 'number' ? v[3] : parseFloat(String(v[3])) || 0,
      activityId: String(v[4] ?? ''),
      accountId: String(v[5] ?? ''),
      categoryId: String(v[6] ?? ''),
      source: String(v[7] ?? ''),
      activityType: String(v[8] ?? ''),
      notes: String(v[9] ?? ''),
    };
  }).filter((r) => r.category !== '');
}
```

```ts
): NativeDrillRow[] {
  const read = spendLinesOrNull(dbPath, startInclusive, endExclusive);
  if (!read) return [];
  const out: NativeDrillRow[] = [];
  const newestFirst = [...read.lines].sort((x, y) => (x.row.date === y.row.date ? (x.row.id < y.row.id ? -1 : 1) : x.row.date < y.row.date ? 1 : -1));
  for (const line of newestFirst) {
    for (const a of line.allocations) {
      const category = topCategoryName(a.categoryId, read.settings);
      if (!category) continue;
      out.push({
        category, date: line.day, account: line.row.accountName, amount: toDollars(a.cents),
        activityId: line.row.id, accountId: line.row.accountId, categoryId: a.categoryId, source: a.source,
        activityType: line.row.type, notes: line.row.notes,
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}
```

### Task 4: Once, rows stored at midnight UTC move to noon

Ruling W2 (Q1b). The pure plan (`shared/date-repair.ts`): exactly 00:00:00 UTC in each form Wealthfolio returns, in a mapped account, not grouped, not overridden; a date-only update that carries the row's own note; batches of 25; a marker that reads back or is "not done". The run (`companion/src/date-repair.ts`): the marker first (unreachable or done: nothing), the mapped accounts, the override ids from the database (unreadable: wait), every account's rows over REST in pages of 500, the batches with a 500 ms pause and a one-row retry of a refused batch, one background bookkeeping entry (`date-repair`, with the moved ids and the rows left alone), and the marker only when nothing failed. `change-entries.ts` gains both new bookkeeping steps (Task 5 uses `sync-bookkeeping`). Wired right after the sync, before the filings and the cube.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/date-repair.ts`, `shared/date-repair.test.ts`, `companion/src/date-repair.ts`, `companion/src/date-repair.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/change-entries.ts`, `companion/src/sqlite-native.ts`, `companion/src/wealthfolio.ts`, `companion/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `bankDayInstant`.
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/date-repair.ts
export const DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY = 'date_repair_done';
export const DATE_REPAIR_BATCH = 25;
export const DATE_REPAIR_PAUSE_MS = 500;
export function utcMidnightDay(stored: string): string | null;
export interface DateRepairRow { id: string; accountId: string; date: string; activityType: string; currency: string; comment: string | null; sourceGroupId: string | null }
export interface DateRepairPlan { moves: Array<{ row: DateRepairRow; write: ActivityWrite }>; skippedGrouped: number; skippedOverride: number }
export function dateRepairWrite(row: DateRepairRow, day: string): ActivityWrite;
export function planDateRepair(rows: readonly DateRepairRow[], opts: { mappedAccountIds: ReadonlySet<string>; overriddenIds: ReadonlySet<string> }): DateRepairPlan;
export function batches<T>(items: readonly T[], size?: number): T[][];
export interface DateRepairMarker { at: string; moved: number; skippedGrouped: number; skippedOverride: number }
export function parseDateRepairMarker(raw: string | null | undefined): DateRepairMarker | null;
// shared/change-entries.ts
export type BookkeepingStep = 'legacy-placeholder' | 'external-marker' | 'transfer-relink' | 'opening-category' | 'transfer-unlink' | 'date-repair' | 'sync-bookkeeping';
// BookkeepingFacts gains: skipped?: number (date-repair), unfiledIds?: string[] (sync-bookkeeping)
// companion/src/date-repair.ts
export interface DateRepairDeps {
  getSecret(key: string): Promise<string | null>; setSecret(key: string, value: string): Promise<void>;
  mappedAccountIds(): Promise<string[]>; searchPage(accountId: string, page: number, pageSize: number): Promise<DateRepairRow[]>;
  overriddenIds(): Set<string>; saveUpdates(writes: ActivityWrite[]): Promise<{ errors: Array<{ action: string; message: string }> }>;
  record(draft: ChangeDraft): Promise<void>; sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>; log(message: string): void;
}
export interface DateRepairResult { ran: boolean; moved: number; failed: number; skippedGrouped: number; skippedOverride: number }
export function runDateRepair(deps: DateRepairDeps, now: Date): Promise<DateRepairResult>;
// companion/src/sqlite-native.ts
```

```ts
import {
  batches, dateRepairWrite, parseDateRepairMarker, planDateRepair, utcMidnightDay, DATE_REPAIR_BATCH, DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY,
  type DateRepairRow,
} from './date-repair.js';
import { bookkeepingChange } from './change-entries.js';

const row = (over: Partial<DateRepairRow> = {}): DateRepairRow => ({
  id: 'a1', accountId: 'wf-chk', date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', activityType: 'WITHDRAWAL', currency: 'USD',
  comment: 'RENT PORTAL · TRN-1', sourceGroupId: null, ...over,
});
const opts = { mappedAccountIds: new Set(['wf-chk', 'wf-visa']), overriddenIds: new Set<string>() };

describe('utcMidnightDay — exactly 00:00:00 UTC, in each form Wealthfolio returns', () => {
  it.each([
    ['2026-10-01', '2026-10-01'], ['2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', '2026-10-01'], ['2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', '2026-10-01'],
    ['2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-10-01'], [' 2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00 ', '2026-10-01'],

    '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', '2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00', '2026-10-01T00:00:01Z', '2026-10-01T00:00:00-04:00', '2026-10-01T00:00:00.5Z', 'soon', '',
  ])('%s is not', (stored) => expect(utcMidnightDay(stored)).toBeNull());
});

describe('planDateRepair', () => {
  it('moves a mapped, ungrouped row at 00:00 UTC to noon on its date, date only, with its own note', () => {
    expect(planDateRepair([row()], opts)).toEqual({
      moves: [{ row: row(), write: { id: 'a1', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'WITHDRAWAL', activityDate: '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', currency: 'USD', comment: 'RENT PORTAL · TRN-1' } }],
      skippedGrouped: 0, skippedOverride: 0,
    });
    // Nothing else is sent: Wealthfolio's update keeps the asset, amount, subtype, metadata and filings it is not sent.
    expect(Object.keys(dateRepairWrite(row(), '2026-10-01')).sort()).toEqual(['accountId', 'activityDate', 'activityType', 'comment', 'currency', 'id']);
    expect(dateRepairWrite(row({ comment: null }), '2026-10-01').comment).toBe('');
  });

  it('skips a row in a group — a linked transfer keeps its date, and its partner’s', () => {
    const plan = planDateRepair([row({ sourceGroupId: 'g-1' }), row({ id: 'a2', sourceGroupId: '' })], opts);
    expect(plan.moves.map((m) => m.row.id)).toEqual(['a2']);
    expect(plan.skippedGrouped).toBe(1);
  });

  it('skips a row whose type Wealthfolio overrides: the search shows only the effective type', () => {
    const plan = planDateRepair([row()], { ...opts, overriddenIds: new Set(['a1']) });
    expect(plan).toMatchObject({ moves: [], skippedOverride: 1 });
  });

  it('never touches a row at any other instant, or in an account the add-on does not map', () => {
    const plan = planDateRepair([
      row({ id: 'noon', date: '2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00' }),
      row({ id: 'local', date: '2026-10-01T04:00:00+00:00' }),
      row({ id: 'second', date: '2026-10-01T00:00:01+00:00' }),
      row({ id: 'elsewhere', accountId: 'wf-brokerage' }),
      row({ id: 'bare', date: '2026-10-02' }),
    ], opts);
    expect(plan.moves.map((m) => [m.row.id, m.write.activityDate])).toEqual([['bare', '2026-10-02T12:00:00Z']]);
    expect(plan.skippedGrouped + plan.skippedOverride).toBe(0);
  });

  it('writes in batches', () => {
    expect(DATE_REPAIR_BATCH).toBe(25);
    expect(batches([1, 2, 3, 4, 5], 2)).toEqual([[1, 2], [3, 4], [5]]);
    expect(batches(Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => i)).map((b) => b.length)).toEqual([25, 25, 10]);
    expect(batches([])).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('the done marker and the change log', () => {
  it('reads a marker it wrote, and anything else as not done', () => {
    expect(DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY).toBe('date_repair_done');
    expect(parseDateRepairMarker(JSON.stringify({ at: '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', moved: 12, skippedGrouped: 3, skippedOverride: 0 })))
      .toEqual({ at: '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', moved: 12, skippedGrouped: 3, skippedOverride: 0 });
    for (const raw of [null, undefined, '', '{oops', '[]', '"done"', JSON.stringify({ moved: 1 })]) expect(parseDateRepairMarker(raw)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('records one background bookkeeping entry carrying the counts', () => {
    const d = bookkeepingChange({ step: 'date-repair', activityIds: ['a1', 'a2'], skipped: 3 });
    expect(d).toMatchObject({
      actor: 'auto', kind: 'bookkeeping', undoable: false, subject: { activityIds: ['a1', 'a2'] },
      after: { step: 'date-repair', count: 2, skipped: 3 },
      summary: "Moved 2 rows stored at midnight UTC to noon on the same date, so Wealthfolio counts them on the bank's day — 3 linked or retyped rows left as they were",
    });
    expect(bookkeepingChange({ step: 'date-repair', activityIds: ['a1'], skipped: 0 }).summary)
      .toBe("Moved 1 row stored at midnight UTC to noon on the same date, so Wealthfolio counts it on the bank's day");
  });
});
```

**Create `companion/src/date-repair.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { runDateRepair, type DateRepairDeps } from './date-repair.js';
import { DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY, DATE_REPAIR_PAUSE_MS, type DateRepairRow } from '../../shared/date-repair.js';
import type { ActivityWrite } from '../../shared/sync-host.js';
import type { ChangeDraft } from '../../shared/change-entries.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const row = (id: string, over: Partial<DateRepairRow> = {}): DateRepairRow => ({
  id, accountId: 'wf-chk', date: '2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', activityType: 'WITHDRAWAL', currency: 'USD',
  comment: `${id.toUpperCase()} · TRN-${id}`, sourceGroupId: null, ...over,
});

/** Wealthfolio as the repair sees it: its rows by account, its secrets, its writes. */
function fake(rows: DateRepairRow[], over: Partial<DateRepairDeps> = {}) {
  const secrets = new Map<string, string>();
  const writes: ActivityWrite[][] = [];
  const recorded: ChangeDraft[] = [];
  const deps: DateRepairDeps = {
    getSecret: vi.fn(async (k: string) => secrets.get(k) ?? null),
    setSecret: vi.fn(async (k: string, v: string) => { secrets.set(k, v); }),
    mappedAccountIds: vi.fn(async () => ['wf-chk', 'wf-visa']),
    searchPage: vi.fn(async (accountId: string, page: number, pageSize: number) =>
      rows.filter((r) => r.accountId === accountId).slice(page * pageSize, (page + 1) * pageSize)),
    overriddenIds: vi.fn(() => new Set<string>()),
    saveUpdates: vi.fn(async (w: ActivityWrite[]) => {
      writes.push(w);
      for (const u of w) {
        const r = rows.find((x) => x.id === u.id);
        if (r) r.date = u.activityDate.replace('Z', '+00:00');
      }
      return { errors: [] };
    }),
    record: vi.fn(async (d: ChangeDraft) => { recorded.push(d); }),
    sleep: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    log: vi.fn(),
    ...over,
  };
  return { deps, secrets, writes, recorded };
}

describe('runDateRepair', () => {
  it('moves every mapped row at 00:00 UTC, skips linked legs, logs one entry and marks itself done', async () => {
    const rows = [row('a'), row('b', { accountId: 'wf-visa' }), row('g', { sourceGroupId: 'g-1' }), row('n', { date: '2026-10-02T12:00:00+00:00' })];
    const f = fake(rows);
    const result = await runDateRepair(f.deps, NOW);
    expect(result).toEqual({ ran: true, moved: 2, failed: 0, skippedGrouped: 1, skippedOverride: 0 });
    expect(f.writes.flat().map((w) => [w.id, w.activityDate])).toEqual([['a', '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z'], ['b', '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z']]);
    expect(f.recorded).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'bookkeeping', after: { step: 'date-repair', count: 2, skipped: 1 } })]);
    expect(JSON.parse(f.secrets.get(DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY)!)).toEqual({ at: NOW.toISOString(), moved: 2, skippedGrouped: 1, skippedOverride: 0 });
    // The grouped leg and the noon row are as they were.
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.id === 'g')!.date).toBe('2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00');
    expect(rows.find((r) => r.id === 'n')!.date).toBe('2026-10-02T12:00:00+00:00');
  });

  it('runs once: a second sync reads the marker and touches nothing', async () => {
    const f = fake([row('a')]);
    await runDateRepair(f.deps, NOW);
    const writesAfterFirst = f.writes.length;
    const again = await runDateRepair(f.deps, new Date('2026-10-15T15:00:00Z'));
    expect(again.ran).toBe(false);
    expect(f.writes).toHaveLength(writesAfterFirst);
    expect(f.deps.searchPage).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2); // one page per mapped account, first run only
  });

  it('after a partial failure, says so, leaves the marker unwritten, and the next sync moves only what is left', async () => {
    const rows = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => row(`r${i}`));
    let refuse = true;
    const f = fake(rows);
    const save = f.deps.saveUpdates;
    f.deps.saveUpdates = vi.fn(async (w: ActivityWrite[]) => (refuse && w.some((u) => u.id === 'r27') ? { errors: [{ action: 'update', message: 'locked' }] } : save(w)));
    const first = await runDateRepair(f.deps, NOW);
    expect(first).toMatchObject({ ran: true, moved: 29, failed: 1 });
    expect(f.secrets.has(DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY)).toBe(false);
    expect(f.deps.log).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Date repair: moved 29 row(s); 1 could not be moved — the next sync tries again.');
    // Paced: a pause between the two batches.
    expect(f.deps.sleep).toHaveBeenCalledWith(DATE_REPAIR_PAUSE_MS);
    refuse = false;
    const second = await runDateRepair(f.deps, NOW);
    expect(second).toMatchObject({ ran: true, moved: 1, failed: 0 });
    expect(f.writes.at(-1)!.map((w) => w.id)).toEqual(['r27']);
    expect(f.secrets.has(DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('does not run without the database (a row’s type override is unknown) or without Wealthfolio', async () => {
    const noDb = fake([row('a')], { overriddenIds: vi.fn(() => { throw new Error('no database'); }) });
    expect((await runDateRepair(noDb.deps, NOW)).ran).toBe(false);
    expect(noDb.writes).toEqual([]);
    const down = fake([row('a')], { getSecret: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('unreachable'); }) });
    expect((await runDateRepair(down.deps, NOW)).ran).toBe(false);
    expect(down.writes).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('leaves a row with a type override alone', async () => {
    const f = fake([row('a'), row('o')], { overriddenIds: vi.fn(() => new Set(['o'])) });
    expect(await runDateRepair(f.deps, NOW)).toMatchObject({ moved: 1, skippedOverride: 1 });
    expect(f.writes.flat().map((w) => w.id)).toEqual(['a']);
  });
});
```

**Create `shared/date-repair.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/date-repair.ts
 *
 * The one-time repair of 1.62 (ruling W2): rows Wealthfolio stored at exactly
 * 00:00 UTC — every bank row the sync wrote as a bare date on 3.8 and 3.9 —
 * move to 12:00 UTC on the same date, the instant new rows are written at
 * (shared/bank-day.ts). West of UTC a midnight-UTC row is the previous
 * evening, so Wealthfolio's Spending page counted a row dated the 1st in the
 * month before.
 *
 * Which rows: in an account the add-on maps (`account_mapping`), stored at
 * exactly 00:00:00 UTC in any of the forms Wealthfolio returns — a bare date,
 * `…T00:00:00Z`, `…T00:00:00+00:00` — and:
 *  - NOT in a group: Wealthfolio copies an updated leg's note and date onto
 *    its partner, and a linked transfer is never spending anyway;
 *  - NOT carrying a type override: Wealthfolio's search answers the EFFECTIVE
 *    type, and an update writes the type it is sent as the stored one.
 * Nothing at any other instant is touched.
 *
 * The write is date-only. Wealthfolio's update (3.8–3.10, storage-sqlite
 * activities repository `update_activity`) keeps what it is not sent — the
 * asset, the amount, fee and quantity, the subtype, the metadata, the status,
 * the review flag, the type override and the group — and category filings
 * live in their own table; a date change leaves split lines alone
 * (`activity_update_invalidates_spending_splits`). Two fields are not patches:
 * the type and currency are required, and the NOTE is written as sent (an
 * update without one clears it), so the write carries the row's own.
 *
 * The companion runs it (companion/src/date-repair.ts) after a sync, in
 * batches, and records it done in an add-on secret only when every row moved,
 * so an interrupted run is picked up by the next sync: a moved row is no
 * longer at midnight.
 */
import { bankDayInstant } from './bank-day.js';
import type { ActivityWrite } from './sync-host.js';

/** Add-on secret: present once every row has been moved. */
export const DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY = 'date_repair_done';
/** Rows per write. */
export const DATE_REPAIR_BATCH = 25;
/** The pause between batches, in ms: Wealthfolio recalculates after each write. */
export const DATE_REPAIR_PAUSE_MS = 500;

const MIDNIGHT = /^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})(?:T00:00:00(?:\.0+)?(?:Z|\+00:00))?$/;

/** The day of a date stored at exactly 00:00:00 UTC, or null. */
export function utcMidnightDay(stored: string): string | null {
  const m = MIDNIGHT.exec(stored.trim());
  return m ? m[1] : null;
}

/** One row as Wealthfolio's search returns it. */
export interface DateRepairRow {
  id: string;
  accountId: string;
  /** The stored date, as returned. */
  date: string;
  /** The type the search returns: the effective one. */
  activityType: string;
  currency: string;
  /** The note; null when the row has none. */
  comment: string | null;
  /** The link group; null or '' when none. */
  sourceGroupId: string | null;
}

export interface DateRepairPlan {
  moves: Array<{ row: DateRepairRow; write: ActivityWrite }>;
  /** At midnight UTC, left alone: in a group. */
  skippedGrouped: number;
  /** At midnight UTC, left alone: a type override. */
  skippedOverride: number;
}

/** The date-only update that moves one row to noon UTC on its day. */
export function dateRepairWrite(row: DateRepairRow, day: string): ActivityWrite {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    accountId: row.accountId,
    activityType: row.activityType,
    activityDate: bankDayInstant(day),
    currency: row.currency,
    comment: row.comment ?? '',
  };
}

export function planDateRepair(
  rows: readonly DateRepairRow[],
  opts: { mappedAccountIds: ReadonlySet<string>; overriddenIds: ReadonlySet<string> },
): DateRepairPlan {
  const plan: DateRepairPlan = { moves: [], skippedGrouped: 0, skippedOverride: 0 };
  for (const row of rows) {
    if (!opts.mappedAccountIds.has(row.accountId)) continue;
    const day = utcMidnightDay(row.date);
    if (!day) continue;
    if (row.sourceGroupId) {
      plan.skippedGrouped += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (opts.overriddenIds.has(row.id)) {
      plan.skippedOverride += 1;
      continue;
    }
    plan.moves.push({ row, write: dateRepairWrite(row, day) });
  }
  return plan;
}

/** `[a, b, c, d, e]` in twos: `[[a, b], [c, d], [e]]`. */
export function batches<T>(items: readonly T[], size = DATE_REPAIR_BATCH): T[][] {
  const out: T[][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i += size) out.push(items.slice(i, i + size));
  return out;
}

/** What the done-marker records. */
export interface DateRepairMarker {
  at: string;
  moved: number;
  skippedGrouped: number;
  skippedOverride: number;
}

/** The stored marker, or null when there is none or it cannot be read — the
 *  repair then runs again, which only ever moves rows still at midnight. */
export function parseDateRepairMarker(raw: string | null | undefined): DateRepairMarker | null {
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const v: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!v || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return null;
    const m = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (typeof m.at !== 'string' || typeof m.moved !== 'number') return null;
    return {
      at: m.at, moved: m.moved,
      skippedGrouped: typeof m.skippedGrouped === 'number' ? m.skippedGrouped : 0,
      skippedOverride: typeof m.skippedOverride === 'number' ? m.skippedOverride : 0,
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

**Create `companion/src/date-repair.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * companion/src/date-repair.ts
 *
 * Runs the one-time repair of 1.62 (shared/date-repair.ts) after a sync:
 * reads every mapped account's rows over REST (the search returns the group,
 * which the add-on's SDK cannot), learns from the read-only database which
 * rows carry a type override, writes the date-only updates in paced batches,
 * records one bookkeeping entry in the change log, and marks the repair done
 * only when every row moved — so a run that stops part-way is picked up by
 * the next sync.
 *
 * Every dependency is injected: index.ts binds the real ones, the tests fake
 * each in its real shape.
 */
import { bookkeepingChange, type ChangeDraft } from '../../shared/change-entries.js';
import {
  batches, DATE_REPAIR_PAUSE_MS, DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY, parseDateRepairMarker, planDateRepair, type DateRepairRow,
} from '../../shared/date-repair.js';
import type { ActivityWrite } from '../../shared/sync-host.js';

/** Page size for the account sweep, and the most pages read per account. */
const PAGE_SIZE = 500;
const MAX_PAGES = 40;

export interface DateRepairDeps {
  /** One add-on secret; rejects when Wealthfolio cannot be reached. */
  getSecret(key: string): Promise<string | null>;
  setSecret(key: string, value: string): Promise<void>;
  /** The Wealthfolio account ids `account_mapping` maps to. */
  mappedAccountIds(): Promise<string[]>;
  /** One page (0-indexed) of an account's rows, as Wealthfolio's search returns them. */
  searchPage(accountId: string, page: number, pageSize: number): Promise<DateRepairRow[]>;
  /** Ids of rows with a non-blank type override. Throws when the database cannot be read. */
  overriddenIds(): Set<string>;
  /** One bulk update; resolves the refusals (Wealthfolio's bulk write is all or nothing). */
  saveUpdates(writes: ActivityWrite[]): Promise<{ errors: Array<{ action: string; message: string }> }>;
  record(draft: ChangeDraft): Promise<void>;
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>;
  log(message: string): void;
}

export interface DateRepairResult {
  /** False when it had already run, or could not run this time. */
  ran: boolean;
  moved: number;
  failed: number;
  skippedGrouped: number;
  skippedOverride: number;
}

const NOT_RUN: DateRepairResult = { ran: false, moved: 0, failed: 0, skippedGrouped: 0, skippedOverride: 0 };

export async function runDateRepair(deps: DateRepairDeps, now: Date): Promise<DateRepairResult> {
  let marker: string | null;
  try {
    marker = await deps.getSecret(DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY);
  } catch {
    return NOT_RUN; // Unreachable: try again after the next sync.
  }
  if (parseDateRepairMarker(marker)) return NOT_RUN;
  const mapped = new Set((await deps.mappedAccountIds()).filter((id) => !!id));
  if (mapped.size === 0) return NOT_RUN;
  let overriddenIds: Set<string>;
  try {
    overriddenIds = deps.overriddenIds();
  } catch (err) {
    deps.log(`Date repair waits: couldn't read the Wealthfolio database (${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}).`);
    return NOT_RUN;
  }
  const rows: DateRepairRow[] = [];
  for (const accountId of mapped) {
    for (let page = 0; page < MAX_PAGES; page++) {
      const items = await deps.searchPage(accountId, page, PAGE_SIZE);
      rows.push(...items);
      if (items.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;
    }
  }
  const plan = planDateRepair(rows, { mappedAccountIds: mapped, overriddenIds });
  const moved: string[] = [];
  let failed = 0;
  const groups = batches(plan.moves);
  for (const [i, group] of groups.entries()) {
    if (i > 0) await deps.sleep(DATE_REPAIR_PAUSE_MS);
    const bulk = await deps.saveUpdates(group.map((m) => m.write)).catch((e) => ({ errors: [{ action: 'save', message: String(e) }] }));
    if (bulk.errors.length === 0) {
      moved.push(...group.map((m) => m.row.id));
      continue;
    }
    // All or nothing: one refused row sends the batch back, so each goes alone.
    for (const m of group) {
      const one = await deps.saveUpdates([m.write]).catch((e) => ({ errors: [{ action: 'save', message: String(e) }] }));
      if (one.errors.length === 0) moved.push(m.row.id);
      else {
        failed += 1;
        deps.log(`Date repair: couldn't move ${m.row.id} (${one.errors.map((e) => e.message).join('; ')}).`);
      }
    }
  }
  const skipped = plan.skippedGrouped + plan.skippedOverride;
  if (moved.length > 0) {
    await deps.record(bookkeepingChange({ step: 'date-repair', activityIds: moved, skipped }))
      .catch((e) => deps.log(`Change log skipped: ${String(e)}`));
  }
  if (failed === 0) {
    await deps.setSecret(DATE_REPAIR_SECRET_KEY, JSON.stringify({
      at: now.toISOString(), moved: moved.length, skippedGrouped: plan.skippedGrouped, skippedOverride: plan.skippedOverride,
    }));
  }
  deps.log(failed === 0
    ? `Date repair: moved ${moved.length} row(s) from midnight UTC to noon; left ${skipped} linked or retyped row(s) alone. Done.`
    : `Date repair: moved ${moved.length} row(s); ${failed} could not be moved — the next sync tries again.`);
  return { ran: true, moved: moved.length, failed, skippedGrouped: plan.skippedGrouped, skippedOverride: plan.skippedOverride };
}
```

```ts
/** The date repair's reads and writes over this companion's client and database. */
function dateRepairDeps(wfClient: WealthfolioClient, store: RestSyncStore, run: string): DateRepairDeps {
  return {
    getSecret: (key) => wfClient.getAddonSecret('simplefin-sync', key),
    setSecret: (key, value) => wfClient.setAddonSecret('simplefin-sync', key, value),
    mappedAccountIds: async () => Object.values((await store.getAccountMapping()) ?? {}),
    searchPage: async (accountId, page, pageSize) => (await wfClient.searchActivities({ page, pageSize, accountIdFilter: [accountId] }))
      .map((a) => ({
        id: String(a.id), accountId: String(a.accountId ?? accountId), date: String(a.date ?? ''),
        activityType: String(a.activityType ?? ''), currency: String(a.currency ?? ''),
        comment: a.comment ?? null, sourceGroupId: a.sourceGroupId ?? null,
      })),
    overriddenIds: () => getNativeOverriddenActivityIds(wealthfolioDbPath()),
    saveUpdates: async (updates) => ({ errors: (await wfClient.saveMany({ updates })).errors ?? [] }),
    record: (draft) => recordCompanionChanges(wfClient, [draft], run),
    sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
    log,
  };
}

```

```ts
  return settings.categoryNames[topCategoryId(categoryId, settings.categoryParents)] ?? '';
```

```ts
/** Ids of every row with a non-blank `activity_type_override` — rows whose
 *  stored type Wealthfolio's search does not show (it answers the effective
 *  one). Strict: throws when the database cannot be read. */
export function getNativeOverriddenActivityIds(dbPath: string): Set<string> {
  const rows = queryNativeDbStrict<Record<string, unknown>>(
    dbPath, 'type overrides',
    `SELECT a.id AS id FROM activities a WHERE TRIM(COALESCE(a.activity_type_override, '')) <> '' ORDER BY a.id;`,
    (parts) => (parts.length >= 1 ? { c0: parts[0] } : null),
    DRILL_CLI_SEPARATOR, SPEND_CLI_MAX_BUFFER,
  );
  return new Set(rows.map((r) => String(Object.values(r)[0])));
}
```

```ts
  rows?: number;
  /** `date-repair` only: the rows at 00:00 UTC it left alone — half of a
   *  linked transfer, or a type Wealthfolio overrides. */
  skipped?: number;
  /** `sync-bookkeeping` only: the rows taken back out of the category (no
   *  longer bookkeeping); `activityIds` are the rows filed. */
  unfiledIds?: string[];
}

/** `sync-bookkeeping`: what one pass filed and took out. */
function syncBookkeepingSummary(filed: number, unfiled: number, category: string): string {
  if (filed > 0 && unfiled > 0) {
    return `Filed ${filed} bookkeeping ${plural(filed, 'row', 'rows')} under "${category}" and took ${unfiled} that ${plural(unfiled, 'is', 'are')} no longer bookkeeping out`;
  }
  if (unfiled > 0) return `Took ${unfiled} ${plural(unfiled, 'row', 'rows')} that ${plural(unfiled, 'is', 'are')} no longer bookkeeping out of "${category}"`;
  return `Filed ${filed} bookkeeping ${plural(filed, 'row', 'rows')} under "${category}", which Wealthfolio leaves out of spending`;
}
```
