### Task 5: The sync's bookkeeping rows stay out of spending

Ruling W3 (Q1c). The pure plan (`shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts`): a row with one of the sync's three markers, in Wealthfolio's spending bucket (`isSpendingBucket` — so a linked leg, money in, or a 3.10 card row written as TRANSFER_OUT is never filed), with no spending filing, and not a starting balance whose account's opening filing was undone, is filed; a row under "Sync bookkeeping" whose marker is gone is taken out. The pass (`companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts`): nothing below Wealthfolio 3.9.0 (`supportsExclusion`, now exported from the opening-debt filer); the candidates and their filings from the read-only database, in the accounts the Spending page reads; the category created with its own description when there is something to file (`createSpendingCategory` takes one) and appended to the excluded categories; each filing on its own; one background `sync-bookkeeping` entry per pass. Wired right after the opening-debt filing, before the cube.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts`, `shared/bookkeeping-filing.test.ts`, `companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts`, `companion/src/bookkeeping-category.test.ts`
- Modify: `companion/src/opening-balance-category.ts`, `companion/src/wealthfolio.ts`, `companion/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `isSpendingBucket`, `SPENDING_TAXONOMY`, `getNativeSpendingSettings`; Task 4's `sync-bookkeeping` step.
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts
export const SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY = 'Sync bookkeeping';
export const SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_DESCRIPTION: string;
export function isSyncBookkeepingNote(notes: string): boolean;
export interface BookkeepingRow { id: string; accountType: string; type: string; typeOverride?: string; subtype: string; groupId: string; notes: string; spendingCategoryIds: string[] }
export interface BookkeepingPlan { file: string[]; unfile: string[] }
export function planBookkeepingFiling(rows: readonly BookkeepingRow[], opts: { categoryId: string | null; noOpeningFile?: (sfinAccountId: string) => boolean }): BookkeepingPlan;
// companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts
export interface BookkeepingFilingDeps {
  dbPath: string; createCategory: (name: string, description: string) => Promise<string>; setExcluded: (categoryIds: string[]) => Promise<void>;
  assign: (activityId: string, categoryId: string) => Promise<void>; unassign: (activityId: string) => Promise<void>;
  appVersion: () => Promise<string | null>; noOpeningFile?: (sfinAccountId: string) => boolean; record?: (draft: ChangeDraft) => void; log: (message: string) => void;
}
export function fileSyncBookkeeping(deps: BookkeepingFilingDeps): Promise<{ filed: number; unfiled: number }>;
// companion/src/opening-balance-category.ts
export const supportsExclusion: (v: string | null | undefined) => boolean;
// companion/src/wealthfolio.ts
createSpendingCategory(name: string, description?: string): Promise<string>;   // default: the opening-balances description
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { isSyncBookkeepingNote, planBookkeepingFiling, SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY, type BookkeepingRow } from './bookkeeping-filing.js';
import { neutralAdjustmentFields, startingBalanceFields } from './sync-core.js';
import { bookkeepingChange } from './change-entries.js';

const CAT = 'cat-sync';
const row = (over: Partial<BookkeepingRow> = {}): BookkeepingRow => ({
  id: 'r', accountType: 'CASH', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', subtype: '', groupId: '', notes: '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-1',
  spendingCategoryIds: [], ...over,
});
const plan = (rows: BookkeepingRow[], categoryId: string | null = CAT) => planBookkeepingFiling(rows, { categoryId });

describe('the sync’s bookkeeping rows, by marker', () => {
  it('knows its three notes and nothing else', () => {
    expect(SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY).toBe('Sync bookkeeping');
    expect(isSyncBookkeepingNote('↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-1')).toBe(true);
    expect(isSyncBookkeepingNote('Balance adjustment · sfin-1 · 2026-10-02')).toBe(true);
    expect(isSyncBookkeepingNote('Starting balance · sfin-1')).toBe(true);
    expect(isSyncBookkeepingNote('ZELLE TO MOM · TRN-1')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('planBookkeepingFiling', () => {
  it('files a spending-bucket placeholder that has no filing', () => {
    expect(plan([row({ id: 'out' })])).toEqual({ file: ['out'], unfile: [] });
  });

  it('files every money-out shape the sync writes, on cash and on a card, and no money-in one', () => {
    const shapes: BookkeepingRow[] = [];
    for (const accountType of ['CASH', 'CREDIT_CARD']) {
      for (const signed of [-50, 50]) {
        const adj = neutralAdjustmentFields(accountType, signed);
        const sb = startingBalanceFields(accountType, signed);
        shapes.push(row({ id: `adj-${accountType}-${signed}`, accountType, type: adj.activityType, notes: 'Balance adjustment · sfin-1 · 2026-10-02' }));
        shapes.push(row({ id: `sb-${accountType}-${signed}`, accountType, type: sb.activityType, notes: 'Starting balance · sfin-1' }));
        shapes.push(row({ id: `ph-${accountType}-${signed}`, accountType, type: signed < 0 ? adj.activityType : 'TRANSFER_IN' }));
      }
    }
    expect(plan(shapes).file.sort()).toEqual([
      'adj-CASH--50', 'adj-CREDIT_CARD--50', 'ph-CASH--50', 'ph-CREDIT_CARD--50', 'sb-CASH--50', 'sb-CREDIT_CARD--50',
    ]);
  });

  it('skips a row Wealthfolio does not count as spending: a linked leg, a card’s transfer out (3.10), an override to money in', () => {
    expect(plan([
      row({ id: 'linked', groupId: 'g-1' }),
      row({ id: 'card-out-310', accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT' }),
      row({ id: 'retyped', type: 'TRANSFER_OUT', typeOverride: 'TRANSFER_IN' }),
    ])).toEqual({ file: [], unfile: [] });
  });

  it('leaves a row filed by hand, by a rule or by the opening-balance filing alone', () => {
    expect(plan([row({ spendingCategoryIds: ['cat-groceries'] }), row({ id: 'opening', notes: 'Starting balance · sfin-c', accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD', type: 'WITHDRAWAL', spendingCategoryIds: ['cat-opening'] })]))
      .toEqual({ file: [], unfile: [] });
  });

  it('takes out a row filed under it that is no longer bookkeeping — a placeholder that expired — and keeps one that still is', () => {
    expect(plan([
      row({ id: 'expired', type: 'WITHDRAWAL', notes: 'ZELLE TO MOM · TRN-1', spendingCategoryIds: [CAT] }),
      row({ id: 'still', spendingCategoryIds: [CAT] }),
      row({ id: 'theirs', type: 'WITHDRAWAL', notes: 'KROGER · TRN-2', spendingCategoryIds: ['cat-groceries'] }),
    ])).toEqual({ file: [], unfile: ['expired'] });
  });

  it('keeps a starting balance unfiled when the person undid its account’s opening filing', () => {
    const rows = [row({ id: 'sb', notes: 'Starting balance · sfin-chk', type: 'WITHDRAWAL' })];
    expect(planBookkeepingFiling(rows, { categoryId: CAT, noOpeningFile: (id) => id === 'sfin-chk' }).file).toEqual([]);
    expect(planBookkeepingFiling(rows, { categoryId: CAT, noOpeningFile: () => false }).file).toEqual(['sb']);
  });

  it('files before the category exists, and takes out nothing then', () => {
    expect(plan([row({ id: 'out' }), row({ id: 'x', notes: 'KROGER · TRN-2', spendingCategoryIds: ['cat-groceries'] })], null)).toEqual({ file: ['out'], unfile: [] });
  });
});

describe('one change-log entry per pass', () => {
  it('names what it filed and what it took out', () => {
    expect(bookkeepingChange({ step: 'sync-bookkeeping', activityIds: ['a', 'b'], unfiledIds: [], categoryName: 'Sync bookkeeping' })).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'bookkeeping', undoable: false, subject: { activityIds: ['a', 'b'] }, after: { step: 'sync-bookkeeping', count: 2, unfiled: 0 },
      summary: 'Filed 2 bookkeeping rows under "Sync bookkeeping", which Wealthfolio leaves out of spending',
    });
    expect(bookkeepingChange({ step: 'sync-bookkeeping', activityIds: [], unfiledIds: ['c'], categoryName: 'Sync bookkeeping' }).summary)
      .toBe('Took 1 row that is no longer bookkeeping out of "Sync bookkeeping"');
    expect(bookkeepingChange({ step: 'sync-bookkeeping', activityIds: ['a'], unfiledIds: ['c', 'd'], categoryName: 'Sync bookkeeping' }))
      .toMatchObject({ subject: { activityIds: ['a', 'c', 'd'] }, summary: 'Filed 1 bookkeeping row under "Sync bookkeeping" and took 2 that are no longer bookkeeping out' });
  });
});
```

**Create `companion/src/bookkeeping-category.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { fileSyncBookkeeping, type BookkeepingFilingDeps } from './bookkeeping-category.js';
import { SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_DESCRIPTION } from '../../shared/bookkeeping-filing.js';
import type { ChangeDraft } from '../../shared/change-entries.js';

const made: string[] = [];
afterEach(() => { for (const d of made.splice(0)) rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

/** Wealthfolio's tables: a checking account and a card the Spending page reads, savings it does not. */
function database(rows: string, settings = ''): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'sfin-bk-'));
  made.push(dir);
  const path = join(dir, 'wealthfolio.db');
  const db = new DatabaseSync(path);
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE accounts (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, account_type TEXT, is_archived INTEGER DEFAULT 0);
    CREATE TABLE activities (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, account_id TEXT, activity_type TEXT, activity_type_override TEXT, subtype TEXT,
      activity_date TEXT, amount TEXT, notes TEXT, source_group_id TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE taxonomy_categories (id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT, name TEXT, parent_id TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE activity_taxonomy_assignments (id TEXT, activity_id TEXT, taxonomy_id TEXT, category_id TEXT, source TEXT, created_at TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE app_settings (setting_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, setting_value TEXT);
    INSERT INTO accounts VALUES ('chk', 'PNC Checking', 'CASH', 0), ('visa', 'Visa', 'CREDIT_CARD', 0), ('sav', 'Savings', 'CASH', 0);
    INSERT INTO app_settings VALUES ('spending.enabled', 'true'), ('spending.account_ids', '["chk","visa"]');
    INSERT INTO taxonomy_categories VALUES ('cat-gro', 'spending_categories', 'Groceries', NULL), ('cat-open', 'spending_categories', 'Opening balances', NULL);
    ${settings}
    ${rows}
  `);
  db.close();
  return path;
}

function deps(dbPath: string, over: Partial<BookkeepingFilingDeps> = {}) {
  const recorded: ChangeDraft[] = [];
  const d: BookkeepingFilingDeps = {
    dbPath,
    createCategory: vi.fn(async () => 'cat-sync-new'),
    setExcluded: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    assign: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    unassign: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    appVersion: vi.fn(async () => '3.10.0'),
    record: (draft) => { recorded.push(draft); },
    log: vi.fn(),
    ...over,
  };
  return { d, recorded };
}

const PLACEHOLDER = `INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('ph', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '600', '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-1', NULL);`;

describe('fileSyncBookkeeping', () => {
  it('files a placeholder under a new "Sync bookkeeping", excludes the category from spending, and logs one entry', async () => {
    const { d, recorded } = deps(database(PLACEHOLDER, `INSERT INTO app_settings VALUES ('spending.excluded_category_ids', '["cat-mine"]');`));
    expect(await fileSyncBookkeeping(d)).toEqual({ filed: 1, unfiled: 0 });
    expect(d.createCategory).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Sync bookkeeping', SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_DESCRIPTION);
    // Appended to the person's own exclusions, never replacing them.
    expect(d.setExcluded).toHaveBeenCalledWith(['cat-mine', 'cat-sync-new']);
    expect(d.assign).toHaveBeenCalledWith('ph', 'cat-sync-new');
    expect(recorded).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'bookkeeping', after: expect.objectContaining({ step: 'sync-bookkeeping', count: 1, unfiled: 0 }) })]);
  });

  it('takes out an expired placeholder, leaves a hand-filed row and one already filed alone', async () => {
    const path = database(`
      INSERT INTO taxonomy_categories VALUES ('cat-sync', 'spending_categories', 'Sync bookkeeping', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('expired', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-09-20T12:00:00+00:00', '40', 'ZELLE TO MOM · TRN-2', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('mine', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '25', 'Balance adjustment · sfin-1 · 2026-10-05', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('done', 'visa', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '9', '↔️ In-transit transfer · PAYMENT · TRN-3', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activity_taxonomy_assignments VALUES
        ('f1', 'expired', 'spending_categories', 'cat-sync', 'manual', '2026-09-20T13:00:00Z'),
        ('f2', 'mine', 'spending_categories', 'cat-gro', 'manual', '2026-10-05T13:00:00Z'),
        ('f3', 'done', 'spending_categories', 'cat-sync', 'manual', '2026-10-06T13:00:00Z');
    `, `INSERT INTO app_settings VALUES ('spending.excluded_category_ids', '["cat-sync"]');`);
    const { d, recorded } = deps(path);
    expect(await fileSyncBookkeeping(d)).toEqual({ filed: 0, unfiled: 1 });
    expect(d.unassign).toHaveBeenCalledWith('expired');
    expect(d.assign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(d.createCategory).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(d.setExcluded).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(recorded.map((r) => r.summary)).toEqual(['Took 1 row that is no longer bookkeeping out of "Sync bookkeeping"']);
  });

  it.each([['3.8.0'], ['3.8.4'], [null]])('does nothing at all on Wealthfolio %s, which excludes no category', async (version) => {
    const { d, recorded } = deps(database(PLACEHOLDER), { appVersion: vi.fn(async () => version) });
    expect(await fileSyncBookkeeping(d)).toEqual({ filed: 0, unfiled: 0 });
    expect(d.createCategory).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(d.assign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(recorded).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('skips rows Wealthfolio does not count as spending, and rows in accounts its Spending page does not read', async () => {
    const { d } = deps(database(`
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('in', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_IN', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '600', '↔️ In-transit transfer · FROM ALLY · TRN-1', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('linked', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '600', '↔️ In-transit transfer · TO ALLY · TRN-2', 'g-1');
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('card310', 'visa', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '5', 'Balance adjustment · sfin-v · 2026-10-05', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('elsewhere', 'sav', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '7', 'Balance adjustment · sfin-s · 2026-10-05', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('credit', 'chk', 'CREDIT', NULL, NULL, '2026-01-01T12:00:00+00:00', '900', 'Starting balance · sfin-c', NULL);
    `));
    expect(await fileSyncBookkeeping(d)).toEqual({ filed: 0, unfiled: 0 });
    expect(d.createCategory).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('keeps a starting balance unfiled when the person undid its account’s opening filing, and goes on past a refused filing', async () => {
    const path = database(`
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('sb', 'chk', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-01-01T12:00:00+00:00', '80', 'Starting balance · sfin-chk', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('p1', 'chk', 'TRANSFER_OUT', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-05T12:00:00+00:00', '600', '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE · TRN-1', NULL);
      INSERT INTO activities VALUES ('p2', 'visa', 'WITHDRAWAL', NULL, NULL, '2026-10-06T12:00:00+00:00', '15', '↔️ In-transit transfer · PAYMENT · TRN-2', NULL);
    `);
    const { d } = deps(path, {
      noOpeningFile: (id) => id === 'sfin-chk',
      assign: vi.fn(async (id: string) => { if (id === 'p1') throw new Error('Activity is not in the spending bucket'); }),
    });
    expect(await fileSyncBookkeeping(d)).toEqual({ filed: 1, unfiled: 0 });
    expect((d.assign as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls.map((c) => c[0])).toEqual(['p1', 'p2']);
    expect(d.log).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining("Couldn't file p1"));
  });
});
```

**Create `shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts
 *
 * Which of the sync's own bookkeeping rows go under "Sync bookkeeping", the
 * spending category Wealthfolio is told to leave out of spending (1.62,
 * ruling W3), and which come back out. Pure: the companion reads the rows and
 * writes the filings (companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts).
 *
 * The sync writes three kinds of row for its own bookkeeping, each known by
 * the note it carries (shared/audit-ledger.ts):
 *  - a transfer in transit (`isPlaceholderNote`): its other half has not
 *    arrived. Money out is a TRANSFER_OUT on cash and a WITHDRAWAL on a card;
 *    money in a TRANSFER_IN on either;
 *  - a balance correction (`isCorrectionNote`): out is TRANSFER_OUT on cash
 *    and WITHDRAWAL on a card; in is CREDIT on cash and TRANSFER_IN on a card
 *    (`neutralAdjustmentFields`);
 *  - a starting balance (`isStartingBalanceNote`): cash in is CREDIT, cash out
 *    WITHDRAWAL; a card's opening debt WITHDRAWAL, a card in credit
 *    TRANSFER_IN (`startingBalanceFields`).
 * Wealthfolio's classifier puts the money-out shapes in its SPENDING bucket
 * (cash TRANSFER_OUT and WITHDRAWAL, card WITHDRAWAL: Expense); TRANSFER_IN on
 * cash is Income, and CREDIT without a refund subtype and TRANSFER_IN on a
 * card are Ignored. Only a spending-bucket row can take a spending category,
 * and only those count as spending, so only those are filed — read through
 * the one definition (shared/wf-spending.ts), so a row Wealthfolio no longer
 * counts (a card's bookkeeping written as TRANSFER_OUT on 3.10, a linked
 * transfer) is never filed.
 *
 * Filed: a spending-bucket bookkeeping row with NO spending filing. A row the
 * person (or a rule, or the opening-balance filing) already filed is theirs.
 * Taken out: a row under "Sync bookkeeping" that is no longer bookkeeping — a
 * placeholder that expired into an ordinary deposit or withdrawal loses its
 * marker, and must be uncategorized like any real row.
 *
 * A placeholder that LINKS is deleted and re-created as a linked pair
 * (`linkPairByRecreate`), and Wealthfolio deletes a row's filings with it
 * (`activity_taxonomy_assignments`, FOREIGN KEY … ON DELETE CASCADE, 3.8+):
 * the new rows start unfiled, and a linked transfer is never spending.
 */
import { isCorrectionNote, isPlaceholderNote, isStartingBalanceNote } from './audit-ledger.js';
import { STARTING_BALANCE_COMMENT_PREFIX } from './sync-core.js';
import { isSpendingBucket } from './wf-spending.js';

export const SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY = 'Sync bookkeeping';
export const SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_DESCRIPTION =
  "Rows the sync writes for its own bookkeeping: transfers waiting for their other half, balance corrections, starting balances. Not spending.";

/** True for a note the sync writes on its own bookkeeping rows. */
export function isSyncBookkeepingNote(notes: string): boolean {
  return isPlaceholderNote(notes) || isCorrectionNote(notes) || isStartingBalanceNote(notes);
}

/** One row as the pass reads it. */
export interface BookkeepingRow {
  id: string;
  /** Upper-case. */
  accountType: string;
  /** Upper-case. */
  type: string;
  typeOverride?: string;
  /** Upper-case; '' when none. */
  subtype: string;
  /** '' when none. */
  groupId: string;
  notes: string;
  /** Its spending-taxonomy filings' categories. */
  spendingCategoryIds: string[];
}

export interface BookkeepingPlan {
  /** Rows to file under "Sync bookkeeping". */
  file: string[];
  /** Rows to take back out of it. */
  unfile: string[];
}

export function planBookkeepingFiling(
  rows: readonly BookkeepingRow[],
  opts: {
    /** "Sync bookkeeping"'s id, when it exists. */
    categoryId: string | null;
    /** True for a SimpleFin account whose opening-balance filing the person
     *  undid (`noOpeningFile`): its starting balance stays unfiled. */
    noOpeningFile?: (sfinAccountId: string) => boolean;
  },
): BookkeepingPlan {
  const plan: BookkeepingPlan = { file: [], unfile: [] };
  for (const row of rows) {
    const bookkeeping = isSyncBookkeepingNote(row.notes);
    if (!bookkeeping) {
      if (opts.categoryId && row.spendingCategoryIds.includes(opts.categoryId)) plan.unfile.push(row.id);
      continue;
    }
    if (row.spendingCategoryIds.length > 0 || !isSpendingBucket(row)) continue;
    if (isStartingBalanceNote(row.notes) && opts.noOpeningFile?.(row.notes.slice(STARTING_BALANCE_COMMENT_PREFIX.length))) continue;
    plan.file.push(row.id);
  }
  return plan;
}
```

**Create `companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * companion/src/bookkeeping-category.ts
 *
 * The post-sync pass of 1.62 (ruling W3): files the sync's spending-bucket
 * bookkeeping rows — transfers in transit, balance corrections, starting
 * balances — under "Sync bookkeeping", a spending category Wealthfolio is
 * told to leave out of spending, and takes back out any row that is no longer
 * bookkeeping. shared/bookkeeping-filing.ts decides; this reads and writes.
 *
 * The same mechanism as the card opening-debt filing
 * (opening-balance-category.ts, which runs first): the category is created
 * in the spending taxonomy when there is something to file, and appended to
 * `spending.excluded_category_ids` (`PUT /spending/settings`). That setting
 * exists only from Wealthfolio 3.9.0 (3.8 ignores it), so on 3.8 — or a host
 * whose version is unknown — the pass does nothing at all: a filing that is
 * not excluded would still count, and would only add a category.
 *
 * Only rows in the accounts Wealthfolio's Spending page reads are considered:
 * Wealthfolio refuses a category anywhere else, and counts nothing there.
 * Each write on its own: one refused filing costs that row, never the pass.
 * One change-log entry per pass (background), when anything changed.
 */
import { existsSync } from 'fs';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import {
  planBookkeepingFiling, SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY, SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_DESCRIPTION, type BookkeepingRow,
} from '../../shared/bookkeeping-filing.js';
import { bookkeepingChange, type ChangeDraft } from '../../shared/change-entries.js';
import { IN_TRANSIT_COMMENT_PREFIX } from '../../shared/reconcile.js';
import { BALANCE_ADJUSTMENT_COMMENT_PREFIX, STARTING_BALANCE_COMMENT_PREFIX } from '../../shared/sync-core.js';
import { SPENDING_TAXONOMY } from '../../shared/wf-spending.js';
import { supportsExclusion } from './opening-balance-category.js';
import { getNativeSpendingSettings } from './sqlite-native.js';

export { SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY };

export interface BookkeepingFilingDeps {
  dbPath: string;
  /** Creates the category (top level, spending taxonomy) and resolves its id. */
  createCategory: (name: string, description: string) => Promise<string>;
  /** Replaces `spending.excluded_category_ids` with this list. */
  setExcluded: (categoryIds: string[]) => Promise<void>;
  assign: (activityId: string, categoryId: string) => Promise<void>;
  unassign: (activityId: string) => Promise<void>;
  /** The host's version (`/app/info`); null when unknown. */
  appVersion: () => Promise<string | null>;
  /** True for a SimpleFin account whose opening-balance filing the person undid. */
  noOpeningFile?: (sfinAccountId: string) => boolean;
  /** The change log: one draft per pass that changed anything. */
  record?: (draft: ChangeDraft) => void;
  log: (message: string) => void;
}

function open(dbPath: string): DatabaseSync | null {
  if (!dbPath || !existsSync(dbPath)) return null;
  for (const uri of [`file:${dbPath}?mode=ro`, `file:${dbPath}?mode=ro&readonly_shm=1`, `file:${dbPath}?immutable=1`]) {
    try {
      const db = new DatabaseSync(uri);
      db.prepare('SELECT 1').get();
      return db;
    } catch {
      /* next form */
    }
  }
  return null;
}

interface Read { rows: BookkeepingRow[]; categoryId: string | null }

/** The bookkeeping rows and the rows filed under the category, with their
 *  spending filings; null when the database cannot be read. */
function readCandidates(dbPath: string): Read | null {
  const db = open(dbPath);
  if (!db) return null;
  try {
    const categoryId = (db.prepare(`SELECT id FROM taxonomy_categories WHERE taxonomy_id = ? AND name = ?`)
      .get(SPENDING_TAXONOMY, SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY) as { id: string } | undefined)?.id ?? null;
    const raw = db.prepare(
      `SELECT a.id AS id, COALESCE(a.account_id, '') AS account_id, UPPER(COALESCE(acc.account_type, '')) AS account_type,
              UPPER(COALESCE(a.activity_type, '')) AS type, COALESCE(a.activity_type_override, '') AS type_override,
              UPPER(COALESCE(a.subtype, '')) AS subtype, COALESCE(a.source_group_id, '') AS grp, COALESCE(a.notes, '') AS notes
       FROM activities a LEFT JOIN accounts acc ON acc.id = a.account_id
       WHERE COALESCE(a.notes, '') LIKE ? OR COALESCE(a.notes, '') LIKE ? OR COALESCE(a.notes, '') LIKE ?
          OR a.id IN (SELECT ata.activity_id FROM activity_taxonomy_assignments ata WHERE ata.taxonomy_id = ? AND ata.category_id = ?)
       ORDER BY a.activity_date, a.id`,
    ).all(`${IN_TRANSIT_COMMENT_PREFIX}%`, `${BALANCE_ADJUSTMENT_COMMENT_PREFIX}%`, `${STARTING_BALANCE_COMMENT_PREFIX}%`, SPENDING_TAXONOMY, categoryId ?? '') as
      Array<{ id: string; account_id: string; account_type: string; type: string; type_override: string; subtype: string; grp: string; notes: string }>;
    const filings = db.prepare(`SELECT activity_id AS activity_id, category_id AS category_id FROM activity_taxonomy_assignments WHERE taxonomy_id = ?`)
      .all(SPENDING_TAXONOMY) as Array<{ activity_id: string; category_id: string }>;
    const filed = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const f of filings) filed.set(f.activity_id, [...(filed.get(f.activity_id) ?? []), f.category_id]);
    const scope = new Set(getNativeSpendingSettings(dbPath).spendingAccountIds);
    return {
      categoryId,
      rows: raw.filter((r) => scope.has(r.account_id)).map((r) => ({
        id: r.id, accountType: r.account_type, type: r.type, typeOverride: r.type_override, subtype: r.subtype,
        groupId: r.grp, notes: r.notes, spendingCategoryIds: filed.get(r.id) ?? [],
      })),
    };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[simplefin-sync] bookkeeping filing could not read the database:', err);
    return null;
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

function excludedIds(dbPath: string): string[] {
  return getNativeSpendingSettings(dbPath).excludedCategoryIds;
}

export async function fileSyncBookkeeping(deps: BookkeepingFilingDeps): Promise<{ filed: number; unfiled: number }> {
  const none = { filed: 0, unfiled: 0 };
  // 3.8 has no excluded categories: a filing there would only add a category.
  if (!supportsExclusion(await deps.appVersion().catch(() => null))) return none;
  const read = readCandidates(deps.dbPath);
  if (!read) return none;
  const plan = planBookkeepingFiling(read.rows, { categoryId: read.categoryId, noOpeningFile: deps.noOpeningFile });
  if (plan.file.length === 0 && plan.unfile.length === 0) return none;
  let categoryId = read.categoryId;
  if (!categoryId) {
    categoryId = await deps.createCategory(SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY, SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_DESCRIPTION);
    deps.log(`Created the "${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY}" spending category for the sync's bookkeeping rows.`);
  }
  const excluded = excludedIds(deps.dbPath);
  if (!excluded.includes(categoryId)) {
    // Appended to the person's own list, never replacing it.
    await deps.setExcluded([...excluded, categoryId]);
    deps.log(`Excluded "${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY}" from spending.`);
  }
  const filed: string[] = [];
  for (const id of plan.file) {
    try {
      await deps.assign(id, categoryId);
      filed.push(id);
    } catch (err) {
      deps.log(`Couldn't file ${id} under "${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY}": ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    }
  }
  const unfiled: string[] = [];
  for (const id of plan.unfile) {
    try {
      await deps.unassign(id);
      unfiled.push(id);
    } catch (err) {
      deps.log(`Couldn't take ${id} out of "${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY}": ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    }
  }
  if ((filed.length > 0 || unfiled.length > 0) && deps.record) {
    try {
      deps.record(bookkeepingChange({ step: 'sync-bookkeeping', activityIds: filed, unfiledIds: unfiled, categoryName: SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY }));
    } catch (err) {
      deps.log(`Could not record the change: ${String(err)}`);
    }
  }
  if (filed.length > 0) deps.log(`Filed ${filed.length} bookkeeping row(s) under "${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY}".`);
  if (unfiled.length > 0) deps.log(`Took ${unfiled.length} row(s) that are no longer bookkeeping out of "${SYNC_BOOKKEEPING_CATEGORY}".`);
  return { filed: filed.length, unfiled: unfiled.length };
}
```

### Task 6: The tripwire in the daily report

Ruling W5's Telegram half (Q1e). `shared/spending-tripwire.ts` compares the two figures the audit snapshot already carries for `now`'s month: within a cent they agree; more is a difference; a figure that was not read, a snapshot that cannot be read, or another month's says nothing. The finding — kind `spending-differs`, a warning, no key — is printed in full (`printsInFull`) as "⚠️ The report and Wealthfolio differ by $X this month — /audit explains", in Brief and Full alike, and cannot be snoozed. `gatherFindings` reads the snapshot and adds it last.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/spending-tripwire.ts`, `shared/spending-tripwire.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/self-check.ts`, `companion/src/daily-findings.ts`
- Modify (tests): `companion/src/daily-findings.test.ts`, `companion/src/index.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the audit snapshot (`AUDIT_SNAPSHOT_SECRET_KEY`, `parseAuditSnapshot`, `AuditSnapshotRead`), `changeMoney`.
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/spending-tripwire.ts
export const TRIPWIRE_TOLERANCE_CENTS = 1;
export type TripwireState = { kind: 'agree' } | { kind: 'differ'; month: string; diffCents: number } | { kind: 'unknown' };
export function spendingTripwire(h: Pick<HeadlineExplanation, 'month' | 'reportCents' | 'wealthfolioCents'> | null | undefined, now: Date): TripwireState;
export function snapshotTripwire(read: AuditSnapshotRead | null | undefined, now: Date): TripwireState;
export function tripwireLine(t: TripwireState): string | null;   // 'The report and Wealthfolio differ by $951.45 this month'
export function tripwireFinding(t: TripwireState): SelfCheckFinding | null;
// shared/self-check.ts — SelfCheckFinding['kind'] gains 'spending-differs'; printsInFull is true for it
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `shared/spending-tripwire.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { snapshotTripwire, spendingTripwire, tripwireFinding, tripwireLine, TRIPWIRE_TOLERANCE_CENTS } from './spending-tripwire.js';
import { formatFindingsSummary, formatSelfCheckBlock, printsInFull } from './self-check.js';
import { isSnoozable } from './findings.js';
import { buildAuditSnapshot, parseAuditSnapshot } from './audit-snapshot.js';
import { explainBalances } from './audit-balance.js';
import { explainHeadline } from './audit-headline.js';
import { healthIssues } from './audit-health.js';

const NOW = new Date(2026, 9, 14, 8, 0);
const h = (reportCents: number | null, wealthfolioCents: number | null, month = '2026-10') => ({ month, reportCents, wealthfolioCents });

describe('spendingTripwire', () => {
  it('agrees when the two are equal, or a cent apart either way', () => {
    expect(TRIPWIRE_TOLERANCE_CENTS).toBe(1);
    for (const [r, w] of [[324351, 324351], [324352, 324351], [324351, 324352]]) {
      expect(spendingTripwire(h(r, w), NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'agree' });
    }
  });

  it('differs at two cents, either way, and says by how much', () => {
    expect(spendingTripwire(h(324353, 324351), NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'differ', month: '2026-10', diffCents: 2 });
    const t = spendingTripwire(h(229206, 324351), NOW);
    expect(t).toEqual({ kind: 'differ', month: '2026-10', diffCents: -95145 });
    expect(tripwireLine(t)).toBe('The report and Wealthfolio differ by $951.45 this month');
  });

  it('makes no claim when a figure is unread, or the comparison is another month’s', () => {
    for (const t of [h(null, 120000), h(130000, null), h(null, null), h(130000, 120000, '2026-09')]) {
      expect(spendingTripwire(t, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
      expect(tripwireLine(spendingTripwire(t, NOW))).toBeNull();
    }
    expect(spendingTripwire(null, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
  });

  it('reads the published snapshot; an absent or corrupt one makes no claim', () => {
    const snap = (reportCents: number, wealthfolioCents: number) => parseAuditSnapshot(JSON.stringify(buildAuditSnapshot({
      balances: explainBalances({}),
      headline: explainHeadline({ month: '2026-10', reportCents, wealthfolioCents, rows: [] }),
      health: healthIssues({}),
    }, NOW)));
    expect(snapshotTripwire(snap(130000, 120000), NOW)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'differ', diffCents: 10000 });
    expect(snapshotTripwire(snap(120000, 120000), NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'agree' });
    expect(snapshotTripwire({ kind: 'absent' }, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(snapshotTripwire({ kind: 'corrupt' }, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(snapshotTripwire(null, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
  });
});

describe('the daily report’s line', () => {
  it('is a warning printed in full — never folded, never snoozed — with the exact words', () => {
    const f = tripwireFinding(spendingTripwire(h(324353, 324351), NOW))!;
    expect(f).toEqual({ kind: 'spending-differs', severity: 'warning', message: 'The report and Wealthfolio differ by $0.02 this month — /audit explains' });
    expect(printsInFull(f)).toBe(true);
    expect(isSnoozable(f)).toBe(false);
    expect(formatFindingsSummary([f])).toBe('*Needs attention*\n⚠️ The report and Wealthfolio differ by $0.02 this month — /audit explains');
    expect(formatSelfCheckBlock([f])).toContain('⚠️ The report and Wealthfolio differ by $0.02 this month — /audit explains');
  });

  it('is not there when the two agree, or a figure was not read', () => {
    expect(tripwireFinding(spendingTripwire(h(324352, 324351), NOW))).toBeNull();
    expect(tripwireFinding(spendingTripwire(h(null, 324351), NOW))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

**Create `shared/spending-tripwire.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/spending-tripwire.ts
 *
 * The tripwire of 1.62 (ruling W5): after every companion sync the audit
 * snapshot (shared/audit-snapshot.ts) carries both spent figures for the
 * month — the report's (the cube's) and Wealthfolio's Spending page's
 * `headline.spent` — and since the report counts spending exactly as
 * Wealthfolio does (shared/wf-spending.ts) they should be equal. More than a
 * cent apart is a finding: the daily report says so in Brief and Full, and the
 * app's status strip shows it. It clears itself on the first sync where the
 * two agree again; it is never snoozed.
 *
 * A figure that could not be read is never read as agreement — and never as
 * a difference: nothing is said. Neither is a snapshot from another month.
 */
import { changeMoney } from './change-entries.js';
import type { HeadlineExplanation } from './audit-headline.js';
import type { AuditSnapshotRead } from './audit-snapshot.js';
import type { SelfCheckFinding } from './self-check.js';

/** Within this many cents the two figures agree. */
export const TRIPWIRE_TOLERANCE_CENTS = 1;

export type TripwireState =
  | { kind: 'agree' }
  | { kind: 'differ'; month: string; diffCents: number }
  /** A figure is unread, or the snapshot is another month's: no claim. */
  | { kind: 'unknown' };

const monthOf = (d: Date) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;

/** Compare the two figures `h` holds, for `now`'s month. */
export function spendingTripwire(h: Pick<HeadlineExplanation, 'month' | 'reportCents' | 'wealthfolioCents'> | null | undefined, now: Date): TripwireState {
  if (!h || h.month !== monthOf(now) || h.reportCents === null || h.wealthfolioCents === null) return { kind: 'unknown' };
  const diffCents = h.reportCents - h.wealthfolioCents;
  return Math.abs(diffCents) > TRIPWIRE_TOLERANCE_CENTS ? { kind: 'differ', month: h.month, diffCents } : { kind: 'agree' };
}

/** The tripwire as the published snapshot says it; unknown when it cannot be read. */
export function snapshotTripwire(read: AuditSnapshotRead | null | undefined, now: Date): TripwireState {
  return read?.kind === 'ok' ? spendingTripwire(read.snapshot.headline, now) : { kind: 'unknown' };
}

/** "The report and Wealthfolio differ by $951.45 this month". Raw; no emoji. */
export function tripwireLine(t: TripwireState): string | null {
  return t.kind === 'differ' ? `The report and Wealthfolio differ by ${changeMoney(Math.abs(t.diffCents))} this month` : null;
}

/** The daily report's finding: printed in full in Brief and Full alike
 *  (`printsInFull`), as "⚠️ The report and Wealthfolio differ by $X this month
 *  — /audit explains". No key, so it cannot be snoozed. */
export function tripwireFinding(t: TripwireState): SelfCheckFinding | null {
  const line = tripwireLine(t);
  return line ? { kind: 'spending-differs', severity: 'warning', message: `${line} — /audit explains` } : null;
}
```

```ts
export function printsInFull(f: SelfCheckFinding): boolean {
  // The tripwire too (1.62): the report disagreeing with Wealthfolio is
  // never folded into a count — the reader must see it, in Brief as in Full.
  return f.severity === 'problem' || f.kind === 'signals-unreadable' || f.kind === 'spending-differs';
}
```

Edit 1 of 1 in `CHANGELOG.md` — replace:

```markdown

## [Unreleased]

## [1.61.0] - 2026-10-14
```

with:

```markdown

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- **The report and Wealthfolio are compared after every sync.** When this
  month's spending totals are more than a cent apart, the daily report says
  "⚠️ The report and Wealthfolio differ by $X this month — /audit explains",
  in Brief and Full, and Home's Accounts tile shows it. It goes away on its
  own once they agree, and a figure that couldn't be read says nothing.
- **The sync's bookkeeping rows stay out of Wealthfolio's spending.** A
  transfer waiting for its other half, a balance correction or a starting
  balance that Wealthfolio would count as spending is filed under a new
  **Sync bookkeeping** category, which the companion excludes from spending
  (Wealthfolio 3.9 and later), and taken back out once it stops being
  bookkeeping. A row you filed yourself is left alone.

### Changed
- **The report counts spending exactly as Wealthfolio does.** The digest's
  "spent this month", the weekly and pool figures, the Budget tab, budget vs
  actual, suggested budgets and the cash forecast now read one definition —
  Wealthfolio's own: the effective type (a type override wins), the subtype
  and the account type; a linked transfer never; a row filed twice counts
  once, under its earliest filing (split lines win when there are any); a
  category Wealthfolio excludes, and its subcategories, left out; an
  uncategorized refund netted; each row placed in its month by its date in
  Wealthfolio's timezone; only the accounts Wealthfolio's Spending page
  reads. Three things now count that used to be left out, because
  Wealthfolio counts them: a charge you dismissed from the needs-a-category
  list (dismissing still keeps it off the list), a charge whose transfer's
  other half is still on its way, and a withdrawal filed under a category
  named "Transfers". On Wealthfolio 3.8, which excludes no category, the
  sync's own bookkeeping rows count too, as they do there.
- **"Why is this different from Wealthfolio?"** now names the one gap left:
  rows on an account Wealthfolio's Spending page reads that the add-on
  doesn't sync. A snapshot from an older companion still shows its reasons.

### Fixed
- **A row dated the 1st no longer lands in the month before.** New rows are
  written at noon UTC on the bank's date instead of as a bare date, which
  Wealthfolio 3.8 and 3.9 store at midnight UTC — the previous evening west
  of UTC. On its first sync after the update, the companion moves the rows
  already stored at midnight UTC to noon on the same date, once, in small
  batches, with one entry in the change log; a linked transfer keeps its
  date. If it is interrupted, the next sync carries on.

## [1.61.0] - 2026-10-14
```

Edit 1 of 1 in `docs/superpowers/follow-ups.md` — replace:

```markdown
    unmount is silent), so it pins "no further reads or writes after unmount".

## Reference: name mismatches between the database, the REST API, and the SDK

```

with:

```markdown
    unmount is silent), so it pins "no further reads or writes after unmount".

## 16. Line up with Wealthfolio (2026-10-14, plan `2026-10-14-line-up.md`)

- **One definition of spending** (`shared/wf-spending.ts`), Wealthfolio's,
  read by every report figure and by the audit's two sides. What it does not
  model: a foreign-currency row (Wealthfolio converts it to the base
  currency at the period's end; the report sums it as stored), and split
  lines that don't add up to the row (Wealthfolio's editor keeps them
  adding up). A type override stored blank is none, as 3.10 reads it; 3.8
  and 3.9 let a blank one override (the row then counts as nothing there).
- **The cube's accounts are still the mapped ones.** A spending account the
  add-on doesn't map is counted by Wealthfolio and not by the cube: the
  audit names it ("On accounts the add-on doesn't sync") and the tripwire
  fires. The digest's own figures cover every spending account.
- **Dismissed charges, and charges whose transfer's other half is on its
  way, now count** in every figure (1.23.0 left dismissed ones out):
  Wealthfolio counts both, and a report that didn't would trip the wire
  every day. Both stay off the needs-a-category list.
- **On 3.8 the bookkeeping pass does nothing** (no excluded categories), so
  in-transit placeholders and corrections that are spending to Wealthfolio
  count on both sides there. An income-bucket placeholder (money in) can't
  take a spending category: Wealthfolio's Income page still counts it.
- **Dates.** A write replayed from what the change log recorded keeps the
  recorded date — Undo of a removed duplicate or a removed row, re-importing
  a starting balance or a correction, moving a starting balance back — so
  one recorded before 1.62 can still land at 00:00 UTC on 3.8 or 3.9. Zones
  beyond ±11 hours still see a noon row a day off.
- **The repair leaves two kinds of row at 00:00 UTC:** a linked leg
  (Wealthfolio copies an updated leg's date onto its partner) and a row with
  a type override (Wealthfolio's search shows only the effective type, and
  an update writes the type it is sent). It needs the database for the
  second, so it waits while the database can't be read.
- **Tripwire timing.** The cube is built moments before Wealthfolio is asked
  for its figure; a write in between shows as a difference until the next
  sync. The cube's months follow the UTC month of the sync; the digest and
  the audit, the companion's local month (unchanged).
- **Left as they were:** `countRulePatternMatches` (a rule preview's count)
  keeps its SQL sign; `companion/tools/audit-month.mjs` and
  `audit-headline.mjs` keep their own copies; the ledger audit's idle-refund
  check still reads the stored type.
- **With the platform branch (U4):** on 3.10 a card's bookkeeping rows become
  TRANSFER_OUT, which Wealthfolio ignores; the pass reads the bucket, so it
  files none of them, and the two compose.

## Reference: name mismatches between the database, the REST API, and the SDK

```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run every new or changed spending test file with the date forced to 2027-01-01**

Nothing may depend on the real clock. A throwaway setup file fakes `Date` before every test; delete it afterwards — none of it is committed.

```sh
cat > zz-2027.setup.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
beforeEach(() => { vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] }); vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2027-01-01T12:00:00Z')); });
EOF
cp zz-2027.setup.ts companion/zz-2027.setup.ts
cat > zz-2027.config.mts <<'EOF'
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
export default defineConfig({ test: { environment: 'jsdom', globals: true, setupFiles: ['./src/test-setup.ts', './zz-2027.setup.ts'] } });
EOF
cat > companion/zz-2027.config.mts <<'EOF'
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
export default defineConfig({ test: { globals: true, setupFiles: ['./zz-2027.setup.ts'] } });
EOF
npx vitest run --config zz-2027.config.mts shared/bank-day.test.ts shared/wf-spending.test.ts shared/audit-headline.test.ts shared/date-repair.test.ts shared/bookkeeping-filing.test.ts shared/spending-tripwire.test.ts src/pages/audit-app.test.tsx
(cd companion && npx vitest run --config zz-2027.config.mts src/wf-spending-agreement.test.ts src/date-repair.test.ts src/bookkeeping-category.test.ts src/daily-findings.test.ts src/sqlite-native.test.ts src/sqlite-native-real-cli.test.ts src/sqlite-audit.test.ts src/ledger-facts.test.ts src/pool-report.test.ts src/report-cube-build.test.ts)
(cd companion && npx vitest run --config zz-2027.config.mts src/index.test.ts -t "tripwire")
rm zz-2027.setup.ts zz-2027.config.mts companion/zz-2027.setup.ts companion/zz-2027.config.mts
git status --short
```
